### apps/server/src/app.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:apps/server/src/app.ts`; SHA-256 `1c5f739e3b20cb7df8571f129d517e1f3fe5547bd7d313cc40d5f0a64e10c0e5`.

~~~~~~text
import Fastify from 'fastify';
import cookie from '@fastify/cookie';
import serveStatic from '@fastify/static';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { DomainError, keys, record, requireThat } from '@chm/rules';
import { Auth, miniIdentity, type Config, type Transport } from './auth.js';
import { Store } from './store.js';

export async function app(store: Store, config: Config, remote?: Transport) {
  const f=Fastify({logger:false,bodyLimit:1024*1024,requestTimeout:15000}), auth=new Auth(store,config,remote);
  const secure=new URL(config.baseUrl).protocol==='https:';
  await f.register(cookie);
  f.addHook('onSend',async(_req,reply,payload)=>{
    reply.header('cache-control','no-store').header('x-content-type-options','nosniff').header('referrer-policy','no-referrer');
    reply.header('content-security-policy',"default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' https://telegram.org; style-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'self' https://web.telegram.org https://*.telegram.org; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'");
    return payload;
  });
  f.setErrorHandler((e,_req,reply)=>{
    const clientStatus=e && typeof e==='object' && 'statusCode' in e && typeof e.statusCode==='number' && Number.isInteger(e.statusCode) && e.statusCode>=400 && e.statusCode<500 ? e.statusCode : undefined;
    const status=e instanceof DomainError ? e.status : clientStatus??500;
    const publicCode=status===413?'REQUEST_TOO_LARGE':status===415?'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE':status<500?'INVALID_REQUEST':'SERVER_ERROR';
    reply.code(status).send({error:e instanceof DomainError ? e.code : publicCode});
  });
  function setSession(reply: import('fastify').FastifyReply, raw: string) {
    reply.setCookie('chm_session',raw,{path:'/',httpOnly:true,secure,sameSite:'lax',maxAge:43200});
  }
  function origin(req: import('fastify').FastifyRequest) { requireThat(req.headers.origin===config.baseUrl,'ORIGIN_REJECTED',403); }
  function session(req: import('fastify').FastifyRequest, mutate=false) {
    requireThat(config.botToken || (config.clientId && config.clientSecret),'UNAUTHORIZED',401);
    const raw=req.cookies.chm_session, s=raw?store.session(auth.hash(raw),Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)):undefined;
    requireThat(s?.owner,'UNAUTHORIZED',401);
    if (mutate) { origin(req); requireThat(req.headers['x-csrf-token']===s.csrf,'CSRF_REJECTED',403); }
    return s as typeof s & {owner:string};
  }
  f.get('/health',async(_req,reply)=>{ try { store.health(); return {status:'ok'}; } catch { return reply.code(503).send({status:'unavailable'}); } });
  f.get('/version',async()=>({version:config.version}));
  f.get('/api/config',async()=>({oidc:!!(config.clientId&&config.clientSecret),mini:!!config.botToken}));
  f.get('/api/session',async req=>({csrf:session(req).csrf}));
  f.get('/api/state',async req=>store.summary(session(req).owner));
  f.get('/api/inventory',async req=>{
    const q=record(req.query); keys(q,['scope','after','limit']);
    requireThat(typeof q.scope==='string' && (q.after===undefined || typeof q.after==='string'));
    requireThat(q.limit===undefined || typeof q.limit==='string' && /^[1-9][0-9]{0,2}$/.test(q.limit));
    return {items:store.inventory(session(req).owner,q.scope,q.after as string|undefined,q.limit===undefined?100:Number(q.limit))};
  });
  f.post('/api/commands',async req=>store.command(session(req,true).owner,req.body));
  f.post('/auth/logout',async(req,reply)=>{
    const s=session(req,true); store.db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE hash=?').run(s.hash);
    reply.clearCookie('chm_session',{path:'/',httpOnly:true,secure,sameSite:'lax'}); return {ok:true};
  });
  f.get('/auth/oidc/start',async(req,reply)=>{
    const result=auth.start(req.cookies.chm_session); setSession(reply,result.raw); return reply.redirect(result.url);
  });
  f.get('/auth/oidc/callback',async(req,reply)=>{
    const q=record(req.query); keys(q,['code','state']);
    const result=await auth.callback(req.cookies.chm_session??'',q.state,q.code); setSession(reply,result.raw); return reply.redirect('/');
  });
  f.post('/auth/mini',async(req,reply)=>{
    origin(req); requireThat(config.botToken,'LOGIN_UNAVAILABLE',503);
    const body=record(req.body); keys(body,['initData']);
    const owner=miniIdentity(body.initData,config.botToken), result=auth.create(owner,req.cookies.chm_session);
    setSession(reply,result.raw); return {csrf:result.csrf};
  });
  await f.register(serveStatic,{root:resolve('apps/web/dist'),wildcard:false});
  return f;
}
~~~~~~

### apps/server/src/auth.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:apps/server/src/auth.ts`; SHA-256 `02611a4d900c34c2b1d55a386dad2be45ef41df5582287b149eafc5c93efd842`.

~~~~~~text
import { createHash, createHmac, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { createRemoteJWKSet, jwtVerify, type JWTVerifyGetKey } from 'jose';
import { DomainError, integer, record, requireThat } from '@chm/rules';
import type { Store } from './store.js';

export const issuer = 'https://oauth.telegram.org';
export const random = () => randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
export const sha256 = (s: string) => createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('base64url');
export interface Config { baseUrl: string; sessionSecret: string; botToken?: string; clientId?: string; clientSecret?: string; version: string }
export interface Transport {
  jwks: JWTVerifyGetKey;
  exchange: (code: string, verifier: string, config: Config) => Promise<string>;
}
export const transport: Transport = {
  jwks:createRemoteJWKSet(new URL(issuer+'/.well-known/jwks.json'),{timeoutDuration:5000}),
  async exchange(code,verifier,c) {
    const response=await fetch(issuer+'/token', {method:'POST',redirect:'error',signal:AbortSignal.timeout(10000),headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',authorization:'Basic '+Buffer.from(c.clientId+':'+c.clientSecret).toString('base64')},body:new URLSearchParams({grant_type:'authorization_code',code,code_verifier:verifier,redirect_uri:c.baseUrl+'/auth/oidc/callback'})});
    requireThat(response.ok,'LOGIN_FAILED',401);
    const body=record(await response.json()); requireThat(typeof body.id_token==='string','LOGIN_FAILED',401); return body.id_token;
  }
};
export function telegramId(value: unknown): string {
  if (typeof value === 'number') { requireThat(Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value>0,'INVALID_TELEGRAM_ID',401); return String(value); }
  integer(value); return value as string;
}
export function miniIdentity(raw: unknown, token: string, now = Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)): string {
  requireThat(typeof raw==='string' && raw.length<=16384,'INVALID_INIT_DATA',401);
  const params=new URLSearchParams(raw), seen=new Set<string>();
  for (const k of params.keys()) { requireThat(!seen.has(k),'INVALID_INIT_DATA',401); seen.add(k); }
  const hash=params.get('hash'); requireThat(hash && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(hash),'INVALID_INIT_DATA',401);
  params.delete('hash');
  const check=[...params.entries()].sort(([a],[b])=>a<b?-1:a>b?1:0).map(([k,v])=>k+'='+v).join('\n');
  const secret=createHmac('sha256','WebAppData').update(token).digest();
  const expected=createHmac('sha256',secret).update(check).digest();
  requireThat(timingSafeEqual(expected,Buffer.from(hash,'hex')),'INVALID_INIT_DATA',401);
  const date=params.get('auth_date'); requireThat(date && /^[0-9]+$/.test(date),'INVALID_INIT_DATA',401);
  const time=BigInt(date); requireThat(time>=BigInt(now-300) && time<=BigInt(now+30),'EXPIRED_INIT_DATA',401);
  try { return telegramId(record(JSON.parse(params.get('user')??'null')).id); }
  catch { throw new DomainError('INVALID_INIT_DATA',401); }
}
export async function oidcIdentity(token: string, nonce: string, c: Config, t: Transport, now = new Date()) {
  // Keep exp/nbf strict. jose maxTokenAge also forbids future iat, so enforce
  // our separate [-3600,+30] issued-at policy after cryptographic validation.
  const {payload}=await jwtVerify(token,t.jwks,{issuer,audience:c.clientId,algorithms:['RS256','ES256','EdDSA'],requiredClaims:['exp','iat','sub','nonce','id'],clockTolerance:0,currentDate:now});
  const seconds=Math.floor(now.getTime()/1000);
  requireThat(payload.nonce===nonce && typeof payload.iat==='number' && Number.isFinite(payload.iat) && payload.iat>=seconds-3600 && payload.iat<=seconds+30 && typeof payload.exp==='number' && payload.exp>seconds && payload.exp>payload.iat,'LOGIN_FAILED',401);
  return telegramId(payload.id);
}
interface Flow { state: string; nonce: string; verifier: string; expires: number }
export class Auth {
  constructor(readonly store: Store, readonly config: Config, readonly remote: Transport = transport) {}
  hash(raw: string) { return createHmac('sha256',this.config.sessionSecret).update(raw).digest('hex'); }
  create(owner: string|null, old?: string) {
    const raw=random(), csrf=random(), expires=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)+43200;
    this.store.db.transaction(()=>{
      if (owner) this.store.player(owner);
      if (old) this.store.db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE hash=?').run(this.hash(old));
      this.store.db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE expires<=?').run(Math.floor(Date.now()/1000));
      this.store.db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL)').run(this.hash(raw),owner,csrf,expires);
    }).immediate();
    return {raw,csrf};
  }
  start(old?: string) {
    const c=this.config; requireThat(c.clientId && c.clientSecret,'LOGIN_UNAVAILABLE',503);
    const session=this.create(null,old), flow: Flow={state:random(),nonce:random(),verifier:random(),expires:Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)+300};
    this.store.db.prepare('UPDATE sessions SET flow=? WHERE hash=?').run(JSON.stringify(flow),this.hash(session.raw));
    const url=new URL(issuer+'/auth');
    url.search=new URLSearchParams({client_id:c.clientId,redirect_uri:c.baseUrl+'/auth/oidc/callback',response_type:'code',scope:'openid profile',state:flow.state,nonce:flow.nonce,code_challenge:sha256(flow.verifier),code_challenge_method:'S256'}).toString();
    return {...session,url:url.toString()};
  }
  async callback(raw: string, state: unknown, code: unknown) {
    requireThat(typeof state==='string' && typeof code==='string' && code.length>0 && code.length<=4096,'LOGIN_FAILED',401);
    const now=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000);
    const flow=this.store.db.transaction(()=>{
      const s=this.store.session(this.hash(raw),now); requireThat(s?.flow,'LOGIN_FAILED',401);
      const f=JSON.parse(s.flow) as Flow;
      requireThat(f.expires>now && f.state===state,'LOGIN_FAILED',401);
      this.store.db.prepare('DELETE FROM oidc_codes WHERE expires<=?').run(now);
      requireThat(!this.store.db.prepare('SELECT hash FROM oidc_codes WHERE hash=?').get(sha256(code)), 'LOGIN_FAILED',401);
      this.store.db.prepare('INSERT INTO oidc_codes VALUES (?,?)').run(sha256(code),now+3600);
      this.store.db.prepare('UPDATE sessions SET flow=NULL WHERE hash=?').run(s.hash); return f;
    }).immediate();
    try {
      const token=await this.remote.exchange(code,flow.verifier,this.config);
      const owner=await oidcIdentity(token,flow.nonce,this.config,this.remote);
      return this.create(owner,raw);
    } catch { throw new DomainError('LOGIN_FAILED',401); }
  }
}
~~~~~~

### apps/server/src/backup-file.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:apps/server/src/backup-file.ts`; SHA-256 `3c4f6e20f506c7fd135489018128e352c48a7ff855890947fe997be6fd1613cb`.

~~~~~~text
import {closeSync, fchmodSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, openSync, unlinkSync} from 'node:fs';

export async function backupToFile(db: {backup(path: string): Promise<unknown>}, target: string) {
  // better-sqlite3 trims filenames. Exclusive creation and SQLite must address
  // the same path, otherwise whitespace can alias an existing destination.
  if (target !== target.trim()) throw new TypeError('Backup target must not have surrounding whitespace');
  // Exclusive creation prevents overwriting an existing target, including a
  // symlink. Permissions are private before SQLite opens the destination.
  const fd=openSync(target,'wx',0o600), created=fstatSync(fd);
  try {
    fchmodSync(fd,0o600);
    await db.backup(target);
  } catch (error) {
    // Remove only the file this operation created, never a replaced path.
    try {const current=lstatSync(target);if(current.dev===created.dev&&current.ino===created.ino)unlinkSync(target);}
    catch (cleanup) {if (!(cleanup && typeof cleanup==='object' && 'code' in cleanup && cleanup.code==='ENOENT'))throw new AggregateError([error,cleanup],'Backup and cleanup failed',{cause:cleanup});}
    throw error;
  } finally {closeSync(fd);}
}
~~~~~~

### apps/server/src/backup.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:apps/server/src/backup.ts`; SHA-256 `6d07503757b5a3796efdd3b29959319a8c6515298d118cbc34f30167e0ffac3e`.

~~~~~~text
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { Store } from './store.js';
import { backupToFile } from './backup-file.js';
const [source,target]=process.argv.slice(2);
if (!source || !target || existsSync(target)) throw new Error('Usage: npm run backup -- SOURCE NEW_TARGET (target must not exist)');
if (!existsSync(source)) throw new Error('Source does not exist');
const store=new Store(source);
try { await backupToFile(store.db,target); } finally { store.close(); }
~~~~~~

### apps/server/src/main.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:apps/server/src/main.ts`; SHA-256 `90d28e600fd681faec2fe50000f3feaa0a0fd1cf7021b467ce854ae3131c6d61`.

~~~~~~text
import { random } from './auth.js';
import { app } from './app.js';
import { Store } from './store.js';
import { requireThat } from '@chm/rules';
const baseUrl=process.env.PUBLIC_BASE_URL??'http://localhost:3000';
const url=new URL(baseUrl);
requireThat(url.origin===baseUrl,'INVALID_BASE_URL');
const enabled=!!(process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN||process.env.TELEGRAM_OIDC_CLIENT_ID||process.env.TELEGRAM_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET);
requireThat(!enabled || (process.env.SESSION_SECRET && process.env.SESSION_SECRET.length>=32),'SESSION_SECRET_REQUIRED');
requireThat(!enabled || process.env.NODE_ENV!=='production' || url.protocol==='https:','HTTPS_REQUIRED');
requireThat(!!process.env.TELEGRAM_OIDC_CLIENT_ID===!!process.env.TELEGRAM_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET,'INCOMPLETE_OIDC_CONFIG');
const store=new Store(process.env.DATABASE_PATH??'data/cheapshot.sqlite');
const server=await app(store,{baseUrl,sessionSecret:process.env.SESSION_SECRET??random(),botToken:process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN,clientId:process.env.TELEGRAM_OIDC_CLIENT_ID,clientSecret:process.env.TELEGRAM_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET,version:process.env.BUILD_SHA??'development'});
for (const signal of ['SIGINT','SIGTERM']) process.once(signal,()=>{ void server.close().finally(()=>{store.close();process.exit(0);}); });
await server.listen({port:Number(process.env.PORT??3000),host:'0.0.0.0'});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname } from 'node:path';
import { craft, integer, inputInteger, keys, mod, record, requireThat, stats, type Equipped, type Group } from '@chm/rules';

interface Player { owner: string; revision: string; level: string; experiment: string; started: number; spent: string; equipment: string }
interface Undo { consumed: Group[]; output: Group; equipment: Equipped[]; price: string }
export interface Session { hash: string; owner: string | null; csrf: string; expires: number; flow: string | null }
function canonical(value: unknown): string {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return '['+value.map(canonical).join(',')+']';
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') return '{'+Object.entries(value).sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b)).map(([k,v])=>JSON.stringify(k)+':'+canonical(v)).join(',')+'}';
  return JSON.stringify(value);
}
export class Store {
  readonly db: Database.Database;
  private statements = new Map<string, Database.Statement>();
  private sql(query: string): Database.Statement {
    let statement=this.statements.get(query);
    if (!statement) { statement=this.db.prepare(query); this.statements.set(query,statement); }
    return statement;
  }
  constructor(path: string, private afterMods?: () => void) {
    if (path !== ':memory:') mkdirSync(dirname(path), {recursive:true});
    this.db = new Database(path);
    this.db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL'); this.db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON'); this.db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
    const version = this.db.pragma('user_version', {simple:true});
    requireThat(version === 0 || version === 1, 'UNKNOWN_DATABASE_VERSION', 503);
    if (version === 0) this.db.transaction(()=>this.db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE players(owner TEXT PRIMARY KEY, revision TEXT NOT NULL, level TEXT NOT NULL, experiment TEXT NOT NULL, started INTEGER NOT NULL, spent TEXT NOT NULL, equipment TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE groups(owner TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES players(owner), scope TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(scope IN ('original','work')), id TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, tier INTEGER NOT NULL, value TEXT NOT NULL, quantity TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(owner,scope,id));
      CREATE TABLE history(seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, owner TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES players(owner), body TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE INDEX history_owner ON history(owner,seq);
      CREATE TABLE commands(owner TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES players(owner), key TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, result TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(owner,key));
      CREATE TABLE sessions(hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner TEXT REFERENCES players(owner), csrf TEXT NOT NULL, expires INTEGER NOT NULL, flow TEXT);
      CREATE INDEX session_expiry ON sessions(expires);
      CREATE TABLE oidc_codes(hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY, expires INTEGER NOT NULL);
      PRAGMA user_version = 1;
    `)).immediate();
  }
  close() { this.db.close(); }
  player(owner: string): Player {
    this.sql('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO players VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)').run(owner,'0','1',randomUUID(),'0','[]');
    return this.sql('SELECT * FROM players WHERE owner=?').get(owner) as Player;
  }
  group(owner: string, scope: string, id: unknown): Group {
    requireThat(typeof id === 'string', 'INVALID_GROUP');
    const g = this.sql('SELECT id,kind,tier,value,quantity FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope=? AND id=?').get(owner,scope,id) as Group | undefined;
    requireThat(g, 'GROUP_NOT_FOUND', 404); return g;
  }
  put(owner: string, scope: string, g: Group) {
    this.sql('INSERT INTO groups VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(owner,scope,id) DO UPDATE SET kind=excluded.kind,tier=excluded.tier,value=excluded.value,quantity=excluded.quantity').run(owner,scope,g.id,g.kind,g.tier,g.value,g.quantity);
  }
  quantity(owner: string, id: string, quantity: bigint) {
    if (quantity === 0n) this.sql("DELETE FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='work' AND id=?").run(owner,id);
    else this.sql("UPDATE groups SET quantity=? WHERE owner=? AND scope='work' AND id=?").run(quantity.toString(),owner,id);
  }
  inventory(owner: string, scope: string, after = '', limit = 100) {
    requireThat(['original','work'].includes(scope) && Number.isInteger(limit) && limit >= 1 && limit <= 200, 'INVALID_PAGE');
    return this.sql('SELECT id,kind,tier,value,quantity FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope=? AND id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?').all(owner,scope,after,limit) as Group[];
  }
  summary(owner: string) {
    const p = this.player(owner), equipment = JSON.parse(p.equipment) as Equipped[];
    const items = equipment.map(e=>({...this.group(owner,'work',e.id),quantity:e.quantity}));
    const last = this.sql('SELECT body FROM history WHERE owner=? ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1').get(owner) as {body:string}|undefined;
    return {revision:p.revision,level:p.level,experiment:p.experiment,started:!!p.started,spent:p.spent,equipment,stats:stats(p.level,items),lastCraft:last ? JSON.parse(last.body) as Undo : null};
  }
  copyOriginal(owner: string) {
    this.sql("DELETE FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='work'").run(owner);
    this.sql("INSERT INTO groups SELECT owner,'work',id,kind,tier,value,quantity FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='original'").run(owner);
  }
  command(owner: string, raw: unknown) {
    const c = record(raw); keys(c,['key','revision','experiment','action']);
    requireThat(typeof c.key === 'string' && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,128}$/.test(c.key), 'INVALID_KEY');
    inputInteger(c.revision,true); requireThat(typeof c.experiment === 'string');
    const a = record(c.action); requireThat(typeof a.type === 'string');
    // Reject resource-heavy numeric fields before canonicalization or writes.
    if (a.type==='level') inputInteger(a.value);
    if (a.type==='original.put') { inputInteger(a.value); inputInteger(a.quantity); }
    if ((a.type==='equip'||a.type==='craft')&&Array.isArray(a.items)) for (const raw of a.items) {
      const item=record(raw); inputInteger(item.quantity);
      if (a.type==='craft') inputInteger(item.worn,true);
    }
    const payload = canonical(c);
    return this.db.transaction(()=>{
      const p = this.player(owner);
      const previous = this.sql('SELECT payload,result FROM commands WHERE owner=? AND key=?').get(owner,c.key as string) as {payload:string;result:string}|undefined;
      if (previous) { requireThat(previous.payload === payload, 'KEY_REUSED',409); return JSON.parse(previous.result) as ReturnType<Store['summary']> & {createdId?:string}; }
      requireThat(p.revision === c.revision && p.experiment === c.experiment, 'REVISION_CONFLICT',409);
      let equipment = JSON.parse(p.equipment) as Equipped[];
      let createdId: string | undefined;
      switch (a.type) {
        case 'original.put': {
          keys(a,['type','id','kind','tier','value','quantity']);
          inputInteger(a.value); inputInteger(a.quantity); const m = mod(a);
          if (a.id !== undefined) this.group(owner,'original',a.id);
          const id = a.id as string | undefined ?? randomUUID();
          const group={...m,id,quantity:a.quantity as string};
          this.put(owner,'original',group); createdId = id;
          if (!p.started) this.put(owner,'work',group);
          break;
        }
        case 'original.delete':
          keys(a,['type','id']); this.group(owner,'original',a.id);
          this.sql("DELETE FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='original' AND id=?").run(owner,a.id as string);
          if (!p.started) this.sql("DELETE FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='work' AND id=?").run(owner,a.id as string);
          break;
        case 'level': keys(a,['type','value']); inputInteger(a.value); p.level = a.value as string; break;
        case 'reset':
          keys(a,['type']); this.copyOriginal(owner); equipment=[]; p.spent='0'; p.started=1; p.experiment=randomUUID();
          this.sql('DELETE FROM history WHERE owner=?').run(owner); break;
        case 'equip': {
          keys(a,['type','items']); requireThat(Array.isArray(a.items) && a.items.length <= 100, 'EQUIPMENT_LIMIT');
          const seen = new Set<string>(); let total = 0n;
          equipment = a.items.map(raw=>{
            const e = record(raw); keys(e,['id','quantity']); const g = this.group(owner,'work',e.id), count=inputInteger(e.quantity);
            requireThat(!seen.has(g.id) && count <= integer(g.quantity), 'INSUFFICIENT_MODS');
            seen.add(g.id); total += count; return {id:g.id,quantity:count.toString()};
          });
          requireThat(total <= 100n, 'EQUIPMENT_LIMIT'); p.started=1; break;
        }
        case 'craft': {
          keys(a,['type','items']); requireThat(Array.isArray(a.items) && a.items.length >= 1 && a.items.length <= 3, 'SELECT_THREE');
          const consumed: Group[] = [], seen = new Set<string>(), inputs: Group[] = [];
          const before = structuredClone(equipment);
          for (const raw of a.items) {
            const s = record(raw); keys(s,['id','quantity','worn']);
            const g = this.group(owner,'work',s.id), n=inputInteger(s.quantity), worn=inputInteger(s.worn,true);
            requireThat(!seen.has(g.id) && n <= 3n && worn <= n, 'INVALID_SELECTION'); seen.add(g.id);
            const e = equipment.find(e=>e.id===g.id), equipped=BigInt(e?.quantity??'0');
            requireThat(worn <= equipped && n-worn <= integer(g.quantity)-equipped, 'INSUFFICIENT_MODS');
            consumed.push({...g,quantity:n.toString()});
            for (let i=0n;i<n;i++) inputs.push(g);
            this.quantity(owner,g.id,integer(g.quantity)-n);
            if (e) e.quantity=(equipped-worn).toString();
          }
          const {result,price} = craft(inputs), output = {...result,id:randomUUID(),quantity:'1'};
          this.put(owner,'work',output); this.afterMods?.();
          const undo: Undo = {consumed,output,equipment:before,price};
          this.sql('INSERT INTO history(owner,body) VALUES (?,?)').run(owner,JSON.stringify(undo));
          p.spent=(BigInt(p.spent)+BigInt(price)).toString(); equipment=equipment.filter(e=>e.quantity!=='0'); p.started=1; createdId=output.id;
          break;
        }
        case 'undo': {
          keys(a,['type']);
          const row = this.sql('SELECT seq,body FROM history WHERE owner=? ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1').get(owner) as {seq:number;body:string}|undefined;
          requireThat(row,'NOTHING_TO_UNDO',409); const undo=JSON.parse(row.body) as Undo;
          this.sql("DELETE FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='work' AND id=?").run(owner,undo.output.id);
          for (const g of undo.consumed) {
            const current = this.sql("SELECT quantity FROM groups WHERE owner=? AND scope='work' AND id=?").get(owner,g.id) as {quantity:string}|undefined;
            this.put(owner,'work',{...g,quantity:(BigInt(current?.quantity??'0')+BigInt(g.quantity)).toString()});
          }
          equipment=undo.equipment; p.spent=(BigInt(p.spent)-BigInt(undo.price)).toString();
          this.sql('DELETE FROM history WHERE seq=? AND owner=?').run(row.seq,owner); break;
        }
        default: requireThat(false,'UNKNOWN_COMMAND');
      }
      p.revision=(BigInt(p.revision)+1n).toString();
      this.sql('UPDATE players SET revision=?,level=?,experiment=?,started=?,spent=?,equipment=? WHERE owner=?').run(p.revision,p.level,p.experiment,p.started,p.spent,JSON.stringify(equipment),owner);
      const result = {...this.summary(owner),...(createdId ? {createdId} : {})};
      this.sql('INSERT INTO commands VALUES (?,?,?,?)').run(owner,c.key as string,payload,JSON.stringify(result));
      return result;
    }).immediate();
  }
  session(hash: string, now: number): Session | undefined {
    return this.sql('SELECT * FROM sessions WHERE hash=? AND expires>?').get(hash,now) as Session | undefined;
  }
  health() { this.sql('SELECT count(*) FROM players').get(); }
}
~~~~~~

### apps/web/src/main.tsx

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:apps/web/src/main.tsx`; SHA-256 `b0859d485bd2f2f72befe10b80aa25c886486c9873c763337c3ac9b50f9e250c`.

~~~~~~text
import React, {useEffect,useState} from 'react';
import {createRoot} from 'react-dom/client';
import './style.css';
function App() {
  const [config,setConfig]=useState<{oidc:boolean;mini:boolean}>(), [message,setMessage]=useState('Проверяем доступность входа…');
  useEffect(()=>{
    void (async()=>{
      const response=await fetch('/api/config');
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('config');
      const c=await response.json() as {oidc:boolean;mini:boolean}; setConfig(c);
      const initData=(window as Window & {Telegram?:{WebApp?:{initData?:string}}}).Telegram?.WebApp?.initData;
      if (initData && c.mini) {
        const login=await fetch('/auth/mini',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({initData})});
        if (!login.ok) { setMessage('Не удалось войти через Telegram. Откройте Mini App заново.'); return; }
      }
      const session=await fetch('/api/session');
      if (session.ok) setMessage('Вход выполнен. Данные сохраняются на сервере. Интерфейс калькулятора готовится.');
      else if (session.status===401) setMessage(c.oidc ? 'Войдите через Telegram, чтобы открыть свои данные.' : c.mini ? 'Для входа откройте приложение в Telegram.' : 'Вход через Telegram пока не настроен. Личные данные недоступны.');
      else setMessage('Сервис временно недоступен. Попробуйте позже.');
    })().catch(()=>setMessage('Не удалось связаться с сервером. Проверьте соединение и повторите попытку.'));
  },[]);
  return <main><p className="eyebrow">Калькулятор модов Cheapshot</p><h1>Ваш инвентарь.<br/>Ваш эксперимент.</h1><p className="mods" aria-hidden="true">🅾 💢 💚 🧿</p><p role="status">{message}</p>{config?.oidc && <a className="button" href="/auth/oidc/start">Войти через Telegram</a>}<p className="note">Техническая основа сервиса. Полный интерфейс калькулятора ещё в разработке.</p></main>;
}
createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(<React.StrictMode><App/></React.StrictMode>);
~~~~~~

### packages/rules/src/index.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:packages/rules/src/index.ts`; SHA-256 `4e785e2e125bfca995c3943b3bdf53f7973fb7165e8c15a206849304a0f455b5`.

~~~~~~text
export const kinds = ['🅾', '💢', '💚', '🧿'] as const;
export type Kind = typeof kinds[number];
export class DomainError extends Error {
  constructor(public code: string, public status = 400) { super(code); }
}
export function requireThat(ok: unknown, code = 'INVALID_COMMAND', status = 400): asserts ok {
  if (!ok) throw new DomainError(code, status);
}
export function integer(value: unknown, zero = false): bigint {
  requireThat(typeof value === 'string' && (zero ? /^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/ : /^[1-9][0-9]*$/).test(value), 'INVALID_INTEGER');
  return BigInt(value);
}
// Only untrusted command fields use this size bound. Derived valid game
// values may grow past it and continue to use the exact integer() helper.
export const MAX_INPUT_INTEGER_DIGITS = 128;
export function inputInteger(value: unknown, zero = false): bigint {
  requireThat(typeof value === 'string', 'INVALID_INTEGER');
  requireThat(value.length <= MAX_INPUT_INTEGER_DIGITS, 'INTEGER_TOO_LONG');
  return integer(value, zero);
}
export function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  requireThat(value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value));
  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}
export function keys(value: Record<string, unknown>, allowed: string[]) {
  requireThat(Object.keys(value).every(k => allowed.includes(k)), 'UNKNOWN_FIELD');
}
export interface Mod { kind: Kind; tier: number; value: string }
export interface Group extends Mod { id: string; quantity: string }
export interface Equipped { id: string; quantity: string }
export interface Selection extends Equipped { worn: string }
export function mod(value: unknown): Mod {
  const r = record(value);
  requireThat(kinds.includes(r.kind as Kind) && Number.isInteger(r.tier) && Number(r.tier) >= 1 && Number(r.tier) <= 6, 'INVALID_MOD');
  integer(r.value);
  if (r.tier === 1 && r.kind !== '💚') {
    requireThat(r.kind === '🅾' ? ['10','11','12'].includes(String(r.value)) : r.value === '8', 'INVALID_MOD');
  }
  return {kind: r.kind as Kind, tier: r.tier as number, value: r.value as string};
}
const prices: Record<Kind, readonly string[]> = {
  '🅾':['84','252','756','2268','6804'], '💢':['66','198','594','1782','5346'],
  '💚':['54','162','486','1458','4374'], '🧿':['72','216','648','1944','5832']
};
export function craft(input: Mod[]): {result: Mod; price: string} {
  requireThat(input.length === 3, 'SELECT_THREE');
  const first = mod(input[0]);
  requireThat(first.tier < 6 && input.every(m => { mod(m); return m.kind === first.kind && m.tier === first.tier; }), 'INCOMPATIBLE_MODS');
  return {result:{kind:first.kind,tier:first.tier+1,value:((input.reduce((s,m)=>s+integer(m.value),0n)+1n)/2n).toString()},price:prices[first.kind][first.tier-1]!};
}
export function stats(level: string, equipped: {kind: Kind; value: string; quantity: string}[]) {
  let damage = 0n, health = 0n, count = 0n;
  for (const m of equipped) {
    const n = integer(m.quantity); count += n;
    if (m.kind === '💚') health += integer(m.value)*n;
    else damage += integer(m.value)*n;
  }
  requireThat(count <= 100n, 'EQUIPMENT_LIMIT');
  const l = integer(level);
  return {damage:((l*(100n+damage)+99n)/100n).toString(),health:((l*(100n+health)+99n)/100n).toString()};
}
~~~~~~

### scripts/check-measure-pipeline.sh

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:scripts/check-measure-pipeline.sh`; SHA-256 `aad72c26f24de7b0a85ec48d0cd452b36c301e7dc3da95df49ec42963023c901`.

~~~~~~text
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
probe_dir="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$probe_dir"' EXIT
cat > "$probe_dir/package.json" <<'JSON'
{"private":true,"scripts":{"measure":"node -e \"process.exit(17)\""}}
JSON
# Match GitHub's explicit bash shell and the actual measurement pipeline.
if (cd "$probe_dir" && bash --noprofile --norc -e -o pipefail -c 'npm run measure | tee capacity.txt'); then
  echo 'Measurement failure was hidden by the pipeline' >&2
  exit 1
else
  status=$?
fi
printf '{"measurementFailureProbe":"passed","exitCode":%s}\n' "$status"

~~~~~~

### scripts/container-smoke.sh

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:scripts/container-smoke.sh`; SHA-256 `10d9cca937d0648f93dd4e447b8063337cb0f52e0fb30b5f17ddea1f23186d5d`.

~~~~~~text
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
sha="${BUILD_SHA:-$(git rev-parse HEAD)}"
image="cheapshotmods:test-$sha"
tag="chm-smoke-${RANDOM}-$$"
restored="$tag-restored"
data="$tag-data"
backup="$tag-backup"
cleanup() {
  docker rm -f "$tag" "$restored" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  docker volume rm "$data" "$backup" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}
trap cleanup EXIT
build_args=()
if [[ -n "${CODEX_PROXY_CERT:-}" ]]; then build_args+=(--secret "id=proxy_ca,src=$CODEX_PROXY_CERT"); fi
docker build "${build_args[@]}" --build-arg "BUILD_SHA=$sha" -t "$image" .
docker volume create "$data" >/dev/null
docker volume create "$backup" >/dev/null
# Both fresh volumes inherit /data ownership from the non-root runtime image.
docker run --rm --mount "source=$backup,target=/data" "$image" node -e "require('fs').writeFileSync('/data/.initialized','')"
docker run -d --name "$tag" --mount "source=$data,target=/data" "$image" >/dev/null
wait_ready() {
  local container="$1"
  for attempt in {1..30}; do
    if docker exec "$container" node -e "fetch('http://localhost:3000/health').then(r=>{if(!r.ok)process.exit(1)}).catch(()=>process.exit(1))"; then return; fi
    if [[ "$attempt" == 30 ]]; then docker logs "$container"; return 1; fi
    sleep 1
  done
}
wait_ready "$tag"
docker exec "$tag" node -e "Promise.all([fetch('http://localhost:3000/version').then(r=>r.json()),fetch('http://localhost:3000/api/state')]).then(([v,r])=>{if(v.version!==process.argv[1]||r.status!==401||process.getuid()===0)process.exit(1)})" "$sha"
docker run --rm -i --mount "source=$data,target=/data" --mount "source=$backup,target=/backup" "$image" node --input-type=module - seed < scripts/volume-fixture.mjs
docker rm -f "$tag" >/dev/null
docker run -d --name "$tag" --mount "source=$data,target=/data" "$image" >/dev/null
wait_ready "$tag"
docker run --rm -i --mount "source=$data,target=/data" "$image" node --input-type=module - check < scripts/volume-fixture.mjs
# Internal HTTP models the proxy hop; public origin/cookie policy remains HTTPS.
# Synthetic credentials are scoped to this disposable container, never the image.
docker run -d --name "$restored" --mount "source=$backup,target=/data" \
  -e PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://smoke.invalid \
  -e TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=isolated-container-fixture-token \
  -e SESSION_SECRET=isolated-container-fixture-session-secret-32-chars "$image" >/dev/null
wait_ready "$restored"
docker exec -i "$restored" node --input-type=module - http < scripts/volume-fixture.mjs
docker inspect --format '{{.Id}} {{index .Config.Labels "org.opencontainers.image.revision"}}' "$image"
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import {app} from '../apps/server/src/app.js';
import {Auth} from '../apps/server/src/auth.js';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync,statSync,existsSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir,cpus,totalmem} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-measure-')),path=join(dir,'db.sqlite'),store=new Store(path);
const config={baseUrl:'http://localhost:3000',sessionSecret:randomUUID(),botToken:'synthetic-measurement-token',version:'measurement'};
const server=await app(store,config),auth=new Auth(store,config),session=auth.create('111');
const command=(action:Record<string,unknown>)=>{const s=store.summary('111');return store.command('111',{key:randomUUID(),revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action});};
try {
  command({type:'reset'});const start=performance.now();
  store.db.transaction(()=>{for(let i=1;i<=10000;i++)command({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:String(i),quantity:'1'});}).immediate();
  const batchSetupMs=performance.now()-start;
  const million=command({type:'original.put',kind:'🅾',tier:1,value:'10',quantity:'1000000'}).createdId!;
  const huge=command({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:6,value:'9007199254740993',quantity:'9007199254740993'}).createdId!;
  const resetStart=performance.now();command({type:'reset'});const resetMs=performance.now()-resetStart;
  const loadStart=performance.now();let cursor='',count=0;
  while(true){const page=store.inventory('111','work',cursor,200);count+=page.length;if(page.length===0)break;cursor=page.at(-1)!.id;}
  const loadMs=performance.now()-loadStart;assert.equal(count,10002);
  assert.equal(store.group('111','work',million).quantity,'1000000');assert.equal(store.group('111','work',huge).quantity,'9007199254740993');
  const state=store.summary('111'), concurrentStart=performance.now();
  const results=await Promise.all(Array.from({length:10},()=>server.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',headers:{origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf},cookies:{chm_session:session.raw},payload:{key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[{id:million,quantity:'3',worn:'0'}]}}})));
  const concurrentMs=performance.now()-concurrentStart;
  assert.equal(results.filter(r=>r.statusCode===200).length,1);assert.equal(results.filter(r=>r.statusCode===409).length,9);
  const address=await server.listen({host:'127.0.0.1',port:0}),headers={origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf,cookie:'chm_session='+session.raw,'content-type':'application/json'};
  const sampleSize=100,durations:number[]=[];let latest=store.summary('111');
  for(let i=0;i<sampleSize;i++) {
    const began=performance.now();
    const r=await fetch(address+'/api/commands',{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({key:randomUUID(),revision:latest.revision,experiment:latest.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:String(i+1)}})});
    assert.equal(r.status,200);latest=await r.json() as ReturnType<Store['summary']>;durations.push(performance.now()-began);
  }
  const oversizedStart=performance.now(),oversized=await fetch(address+'/api/commands',{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({key:randomUUID(),revision:latest.revision,experiment:latest.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'9'.repeat(1000000)}})});
  assert.equal(oversized.status,400);assert.equal((await oversized.json() as {error:string}).error,'INTEGER_TOO_LONG');const oversizedRejectMs=performance.now()-oversizedStart;
  const bytes=statSync(path).size+(existsSync(path+'-wal')?statSync(path+'-wal').size:0);
  console.log(JSON.stringify({node:process.version,platform:process.platform,arch:process.arch,cpu:cpus()[0]?.model,logicalCpus:cpus().length,hostMemoryMiB:Math.round(totalmem()/1048576),dataset:{distinctGroups:10000,millionGroup:'1000000',exactQuantity:'9007199254740993'},batchSetup:{groups:10000,outerTransaction:true,elapsedMs:batchSetupMs},sequentialHttp:{sampleSize,commitPerRequest:true,totalMs:durations.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),p95Ms:[...durations].sort((a,b)=>a-b)[Math.ceil(sampleSize*0.95)-1]},oversizedInteger:{digits:1000000,rejectMs:oversizedRejectMs},resetMs,paginatedLoadMs:loadMs,tenSameRevisionInjectedRequestsMs:concurrentMs,dbAndWalBytes:bytes,rssMiB:process.memoryUsage().rss/1048576,peakRssMiB:process.resourceUsage().maxRSS/1024},null,2));
}finally{await server.close();store.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
~~~~~~

### scripts/volume-fixture.mjs

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:scripts/volume-fixture.mjs`; SHA-256 `01b2f0def449409e859e6f3db84a2d11ad2268609251b68e51626949df34c676`.

~~~~~~text
import {Store} from '/app/apps/server/dist/store.js';
import {backupToFile} from '/app/apps/server/dist/backup-file.js';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {randomUUID,createHmac} from 'node:crypto';
import {readFileSync,writeFileSync,statSync} from 'node:fs';
const {fetch,URLSearchParams}=globalThis;
const mode=process.argv[2];
if(mode==='http') {
  const origin=process.env.PUBLIC_BASE_URL,base='http://localhost:3000',expected=JSON.parse(readFileSync('/data/expected.json','utf8'));
  assert.equal((await fetch(base+'/health')).status,200);
  assert.equal((await fetch(base+'/version').then(r=>r.json())).version,process.env.BUILD_SHA);
  assert.equal((await fetch(base+'/api/state')).status,401);
  const params=new URLSearchParams({auth_date:String(Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)),user:JSON.stringify({id:111})});
  const check=[...params.entries()].sort(([a],[b])=>a<b?-1:a>b?1:0).map(([k,v])=>k+'='+v).join('\n');
  params.set('hash',createHmac('sha256',createHmac('sha256','WebAppData').update(process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN).digest()).update(check).digest('hex'));
  const login=await fetch(base+'/auth/mini',{method:'POST',headers:{origin,'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({initData:params.toString()})});
  assert.equal(login.status,200);const cookie=login.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0],csrf=(await login.json()).csrf;
  const headers={origin,cookie,'x-csrf-token':csrf,'content-type':'application/json'};
  const get=path=>fetch(base+path,{headers}).then(async r=>{assert.equal(r.status,200);return r.json();});
  assert.deepEqual({state:await get('/api/state'),original:(await get('/api/inventory?scope=original')).items,work:(await get('/api/inventory?scope=work')).items},expected);
  const send=async action=>{const state=await get('/api/state');const r=await fetch(base+'/api/commands',{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action})});assert.equal(r.status,200);return r.json();};
  const undone=await send({type:'undo'});assert.equal(undone.spent,'0');assert.equal(undone.level,'9007199254740993');assert.equal(undone.equipment[0].quantity,'3');
  assert.equal((await send({type:'craft',items:[{id:undone.equipment[0].id,quantity:'3',worn:'3'}]})).spent,'84');
  console.log(JSON.stringify({mode,result:'passed',transport:'HTTP server on restored volume'}));
} else {
const store=new Store('/data/cheapshot.sqlite');
const owner='111';
const command=action=>{const s=store.summary(owner);return store.command(owner,{key:randomUUID(),revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action});};
try {
  if (mode==='seed') {
    const id=command({type:'original.put',kind:'🅾',tier:1,value:'10',quantity:'6'}).createdId;
    command({type:'level',value:'9007199254740993'});
    command({type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:'3'}]});
    command({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:'3'}]});
    const snapshot={state:store.summary(owner),original:store.inventory(owner,'original'),work:store.inventory(owner,'work')};
    writeFileSync('/data/expected.json',JSON.stringify(snapshot),{mode:0o600});writeFileSync('/backup/expected.json',JSON.stringify(snapshot),{mode:0o600});
    await backupToFile(store.db,'/backup/cheapshot.sqlite');assert.equal(statSync('/backup/cheapshot.sqlite').mode&0o777,0o600);
    assert.equal(snapshot.state.spent,'84');
  } else {
    const expected=JSON.parse(readFileSync('/data/expected.json','utf8'));
    assert.deepEqual({state:store.summary(owner),original:store.inventory(owner,'original'),work:store.inventory(owner,'work')},expected);
    const undone=command({type:'undo'});assert.equal(undone.spent,'0');assert.equal(undone.level,'9007199254740993');assert.equal(undone.equipment[0].quantity,'3');
    const id=undone.equipment[0].id;assert.equal(command({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:'3'}]}).spent,'84');
  }
  console.log(JSON.stringify({mode,result:'passed'}));
} finally {store.close();}
}
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {app} from '../apps/server/src/app.js';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import {Auth} from '../apps/server/src/auth.js';
import {config,initData} from './helpers.js';
test('HTTPS sessions set Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite and rotate after Mini App login',async()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:'),secure={...config,baseUrl:'https://fixture.invalid'},f=await app(s,secure);
  try {
    const old=new Auth(s,secure).create('111');
    const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/mini',cookies:{chm_session:old.raw},headers:{origin:secure.baseUrl},payload:{initData:initData()}});
    assert.equal(r.statusCode,200);assert.notEqual(r.cookies[0]!.value,old.raw);
    const header=r.headers['set-cookie'] as string;for(const attr of ['HttpOnly','Secure','SameSite=Lax','Max-Age=43200'])assert.ok(header.includes(attr));
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:old.raw}})).statusCode,401);
  }finally{await f.close();s.close();}
});
test('HTTP authorization, CSRF, owner isolation, exact numbers, retry, conflicts and logout',async()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:'),f=await app(s,config),origin=config.baseUrl;
  try {
    for (const url of ['/api/state','/api/session','/api/inventory?scope=work']) assert.equal((await f.inject({url,headers:{'x-user-id':'111'}})).statusCode,401);
    const login=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/mini',headers:{origin},payload:{initData:initData()}});assert.equal(login.statusCode,200);
    const cookies={chm_session:login.cookies[0]!.value},headers={origin,'x-csrf-token':login.json().csrf},base=(await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies})).json();
    const payload={key:randomUUID(),revision:base.revision,experiment:base.experiment,action:{type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:6,value:'9007199254740993',quantity:'9007199254740993'}};
    for(const h of [{},{...headers,origin:'https://evil.invalid'},{origin}]) assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers:h,payload})).statusCode,403);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload:{...payload,owner:'222'}})).statusCode,400);
    const first=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload});assert.equal(first.statusCode,200);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload})).body,first.body);
    const groups=(await f.inject({url:'/api/inventory?scope=original',cookies})).json().items;assert.equal(groups[0].value,'9007199254740993');assert.equal(groups[0].quantity,'9007199254740993');
    const next=first.json(),level={key:randomUUID(),revision:next.revision,experiment:next.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'9007199254740993'}};
    const competing=await Promise.all([level,{...level,key:randomUUID()}].map(payload=>f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload})));
    assert.deepEqual(competing.map(r=>r.statusCode).sort(),[200,409]);assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies})).json().level,'9007199254740993');
    const second=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/mini',headers:{origin},payload:{initData:initData(222)}}),secondCookies={chm_session:second.cookies[0]!.value};
    const secondState=(await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:secondCookies})).json();
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state?owner=111',cookies:secondCookies})).json().level,'1');
    assert.deepEqual((await f.inject({url:'/api/inventory?scope=original',cookies:secondCookies})).json().items,[]);
    const alien={key:randomUUID(),revision:secondState.revision,experiment:secondState.experiment,action:{type:'original.delete',id:groups[0].id}};
    assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies:secondCookies,headers:{origin,'x-csrf-token':second.json().csrf},payload:alien})).statusCode,404);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies:secondCookies,headers:{origin,'x-csrf-token':second.json().csrf},payload:{...alien,experiment:next.experiment}})).statusCode,409);
    const oversized=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload:{padding:'x'.repeat(1024*1024)}});assert.equal(oversized.statusCode,413);assert.equal(oversized.json().error,'REQUEST_TOO_LARGE');
    assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/logout',cookies,headers})).statusCode,200);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies})).statusCode,401);
    for (const bad of ['', 'chm-forged']) assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:bad}})).statusCode,401);
    const expired=new Auth(s,config).create('111');s.db.prepare('UPDATE sessions SET expires=0').run();assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:expired.raw}})).statusCode,401);
  }finally{await f.close();s.close();}
});
test('real socket response lost after commit; restart preserves idempotency and exact API integers',async()=>{
  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-http-')),path=join(dir,'db');let s=new Store(path),f=await app(s,config);
  try {
    f.addHook('onSend',async(req,reply,payload)=>{if(req.headers['x-drop-fixture']==='yes')reply.raw.destroy();return payload;});
    const address=await f.listen({host:'127.0.0.1',port:0});
    const session=new Auth(s,config).create('111'),cookie='chm_session='+session.raw;
    const headers={origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf,cookie,'content-type':'application/json'};
    async function send(action:Record<string,unknown>) {const state=s.summary('111');const r=await fetch(address+'/api/commands',{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action})});assert.equal(r.status,200);return await r.json() as {createdId:string};}
    const big='9007199254740993',huge=await send({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:6,value:big,quantity:big});
    await send({type:'level',value:big});await send({type:'equip',items:[{id:huge.createdId,quantity:'1'}]});
    assert.equal(s.summary('111').stats.health,'811296384146076004301129887254');
    const six=await send({type:'original.put',kind:'🅾',tier:1,value:'10',quantity:'6'});
    // Original edits after equip do not affect work, so reset to import the new group.
    await send({type:'reset'});const before=s.summary('111');
    const envelope={key:randomUUID(),revision:before.revision,experiment:before.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[{id:six.createdId,quantity:'3',worn:'0'}]}};
    await assert.rejects(()=>fetch(address+'/api/commands',{method:'POST',headers:{...headers,'x-drop-fixture':'yes'},body:JSON.stringify(envelope)}));
    const committed=s.summary('111');assert.equal(committed.spent,'84');
    const replay=await fetch(address+'/api/commands',{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify(envelope)});assert.equal(replay.status,200);const result=await replay.json();
    await f.close();s.close();s=new Store(path);f=await app(s,config);
    const retry=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',headers,payload:envelope});assert.equal(retry.statusCode,200);assert.deepEqual(retry.json(),result);assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),committed);
    const data=(await f.inject({url:'/api/inventory?scope=work',headers})).json().items as {id:string;value:string;quantity:string}[];
    const group=data.find(g=>g.id===huge.createdId)!;assert.equal(group.value,big);assert.equal(group.quantity,big);assert.equal(s.summary('111').level,big);
  }finally{await f.close();if(s.db.open)s.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
});
test('missing configuration, safe errors, database failure and readiness',async()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:');let f=await app(s,{...config,botToken:undefined,clientId:undefined,clientSecret:undefined});
  try {
    assert.deepEqual((await f.inject('/api/config')).json(),{oidc:false,mini:false});assert.equal((await f.inject('/auth/oidc/start')).statusCode,503);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/mini',headers:{origin:config.baseUrl},payload:{initData:initData()}})).statusCode,503);
    assert.deepEqual((await f.inject('/version')).json(),{version:'a'.repeat(40)});assert.equal((await f.inject('/health')).statusCode,200);
    const auth=new Auth(s,config),session=auth.create('111');
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:session.raw}})).statusCode,401);
    await f.close();f=await app(s,config);
    s.db.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER fixture_fail BEFORE UPDATE ON players BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'fixture-secret-must-not-leak'); END;`);
    const state=s.summary('111');const failed=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies:{chm_session:session.raw},headers:{origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf},payload:{key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'5'}}});
    assert.equal(failed.statusCode,500);assert.deepEqual(failed.json(),{error:'SERVER_ERROR'});assert.equal(s.summary('111').level,'1');
    s.close();assert.equal((await f.inject('/health')).statusCode,503);
  }finally{await f.close();if(s.db.open)s.close();}
});
test('Fastify parser errors retain safe 4xx and never mutate saved state',async()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:'),f=await app(s,config),session=new Auth(s,config).create('111');
  const headers={origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf},cookies={chm_session:session.raw};
  try {
    const before=s.summary('111');
    for (const [type,body,status,error] of [
      ['application/json','{',400,'INVALID_REQUEST'],
      ['application/xml','<command/>',415,'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE'],
      ['text/plain','not-a-command',400,'INVALID_COMMAND']
    ] as const) {
      const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers:{...headers,'content-type':type},payload:body});
      assert.equal(r.statusCode,status);assert.deepEqual(r.json(),{error});assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);
    }
    assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get() as {n:number}).n,0);
  } finally {await f.close();s.close();}
});
test('untrusted numeric fields are bounded before conversion; oversized request leaves both players usable',async()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:'),f=await app(s,config),session=new Auth(s,config).create('111');
  const headers={origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf},cookies={chm_session:session.raw};
  const envelope=(action:Record<string,unknown>,revision=s.summary('111').revision)=>({key:randomUUID(),revision,experiment:s.summary('111').experiment,action});

    const created=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload:envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:'9'.repeat(128),quantity:'9'.repeat(128)})});assert.equal(created.statusCode,200);
    const id=created.json().createdId,before=s.summary('111'),count=(s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get() as {n:number}).n,over='9'.repeat(129);
    const cases=[envelope({type:'level',value:over}),envelope({type:'level',value:'2'},over),envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:over,quantity:'1'}),envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:'1',quantity:over}),envelope({type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:over}]}),envelope({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:over}]}),envelope({type:'level',value:'9'.repeat(1000000)})];
    for (const payload of cases) {
      assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(payload))<1024*1024);
      const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload});assert.equal(r.statusCode,400);assert.deepEqual(r.json(),{error:'INTEGER_TOO_LONG'});assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);
      assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get() as {n:number}).n,count);
    }
    const other=new Auth(s,config).create('222'),state=s.summary('222');
    const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies:{chm_session:other.raw},headers:{origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':other.csrf},payload:{key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'200'}}});
    assert.equal(r.statusCode,200);assert.equal(r.json().level,'200');
  } finally {await f.close();s.close();}
});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {generateKeyPair,exportJWK,createLocalJWKSet,SignJWT,jwtVerify} from 'jose';
import {app} from '../apps/server/src/app.js';
import {Auth,miniIdentity,oidcIdentity,issuer,sha256,type Transport} from '../apps/server/src/auth.js';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import {config,initData} from './helpers.js';
const pair=await generateKeyPair('RS256'), jwks=createLocalJWKSet({keys:[{...await exportJWK(pair.publicKey),kid:'fixture',alg:'RS256'}]});
async function token(nonce:string, overrides: Record<string,unknown>={},privateKey=pair.privateKey) {
  const now=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000);
  return new SignJWT({id:111,nonce,sub:'deliberately-different-sub',iss:issuer,aud:config.clientId,iat:now,exp:now+600,...overrides}).setProtectedHeader({alg:'RS256',kid:'fixture'}).sign(privateKey);
}
test('OIDC real signature and every identity/claim validation',async()=>{
  const remote: Transport={jwks,exchange:async()=>{throw new Error('unused');}};
  assert.equal(await oidcIdentity(await token('nonce'),'nonce',config,remote),'111');
  for (const claims of [{iss:'https://evil.invalid'},{aud:'other-client'},{exp:1},{nonce:'wrong'},{iat:Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)+60},{id:undefined},{id:0},{id:9007199254740992},{sub:undefined},{exp:undefined},{iat:undefined}]) {
    await assert.rejects(()=>token('nonce',claims).then(t=>oidcIdentity(t,'nonce',config,remote)));
  }
  const other=await generateKeyPair('RS256');await assert.rejects(()=>token('nonce',{},other.privateKey).then(t=>oidcIdentity(t,'nonce',config,remote)));
});
test('Mini App signature, duplicate fields, missing identity and exact time boundaries',()=>{
  const now=1900000000;
  for (const date of [now-300,now,now+30]) assert.equal(miniIdentity(initData(111,date),config.botToken!,now),'111');
  for (const raw of [initData(111,now-301),initData(111,now+31),initData(111,now)+'&user=%7B%22id%22%3A222%7D',initData(111,now)+'&auth_date='+now,initData(111,now,{user:'{}'}),initData(111,now,{user:'null'}),'auth_date='+now+'&user=%7B%22id%22%3A111%7D']) assert.throws(()=>miniIdentity(raw,config.botToken!,now));
  const tampered=new URLSearchParams(initData(111,now));tampered.set('user',JSON.stringify({id:222}));assert.throws(()=>miniIdentity(tampered.toString(),config.botToken!,now));
  const missing=new URLSearchParams(initData(111,now));missing.delete('user');assert.throws(()=>miniIdentity(missing.toString(),config.botToken!,now));
  const badHash=new URLSearchParams(initData(111,now));badHash.set('hash','0'.repeat(64));assert.throws(()=>miniIdentity(badHash.toString(),config.botToken!,now));
});
test('issued-at skew is independent of strict expiry/not-before and one-hour age',async()=>{
  const seconds=1900000000,now=new Date(seconds*1000),remote:Transport={jwks,exchange:async()=>{throw new Error('unused');}};
  const future=await token('nonce',{iat:seconds+5,exp:seconds+600});
  // Reproduce D1 with the actual pinned dependency before asserting our policy.
  await assert.rejects(()=>jwtVerify(future,jwks,{issuer,audience:config.clientId,maxTokenAge:'1h',clockTolerance:0,currentDate:now}),/iat/);
  for (const delta of [-3600,0,5,30]) assert.equal(await oidcIdentity(await token('nonce',{iat:seconds+delta,exp:seconds+600}),'nonce',config,remote,now),'111');
  for (const claims of [{iat:seconds-3601,exp:seconds+600},{iat:seconds+31,exp:seconds+600},{iat:seconds-1,exp:seconds},{iat:seconds-2,exp:seconds-1},{iat:seconds+30,exp:seconds+20},{iat:seconds,exp:seconds+600,nbf:seconds+1}]) {
    await assert.rejects(()=>token('nonce',claims).then(t=>oidcIdentity(t,'nonce',config,remote,now)));
  }
  assert.equal(await oidcIdentity(await token('nonce',{iat:seconds,exp:seconds+600,nbf:seconds}),'nonce',config,remote,now),'111');
});
test('OIDC callback binds session/state/nonce and PKCE; rotates session and shares Mini App aggregate',async()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:');let expectedChallenge='',nonce='',exchanges=0;
  const remote: Transport={jwks,exchange:async(code,verifier,c)=>{
    exchanges++;assert.equal(code,'single-code');assert.equal(c.clientId,config.clientId);
    if (sha256(verifier)!==expectedChallenge) throw new Error('PKCE rejected'); return token(nonce);
  }};
  const f=await app(s,config,remote);
  try {
    const start=await f.inject('/auth/oidc/start'), url=new URL(start.headers.location!);
    const old=start.cookies[0]!.value;expectedChallenge=url.searchParams.get('code_challenge')!;nonce=url.searchParams.get('nonce')!;
    assert.equal(url.searchParams.get('scope'),'openid profile');assert.equal(url.searchParams.get('code_challenge_method'),'S256');
    const state=url.searchParams.get('state')!, callback='/auth/oidc/callback?'+new URLSearchParams({state,code:'single-code'});
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:callback,cookies:{chm_session:'forged'}})).statusCode,401);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/auth/oidc/callback?state=wrong&code=single-code',cookies:{chm_session:old}})).statusCode,401);
    const finish=await f.inject({url:callback,cookies:{chm_session:old}});assert.equal(finish.statusCode,302);const fresh=finish.cookies[0]!.value;
    assert.notEqual(fresh,old);assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:old}})).statusCode,401);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:callback,cookies:{chm_session:old}})).statusCode,401);assert.equal(exchanges,1);
    const viaOidc=(await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:fresh}})).json();
    const mini=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/mini',headers:{origin:config.baseUrl},payload:{initData:initData()}});
    assert.equal(mini.statusCode,200);const viaMini=(await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:mini.cookies[0]!.value}})).json();assert.deepEqual(viaMini,viaOidc);
    const other=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/auth/mini',headers:{origin:config.baseUrl},payload:{initData:initData(222)}});
    assert.notEqual((await f.inject({url:'/api/state',cookies:{chm_session:other.cookies[0]!.value}})).json().experiment,viaMini.experiment);
    assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM players').get() as {n:number}).n,2);
    // Reusing a code with a new otherwise valid state is refused before token exchange.
    const second=await f.inject('/auth/oidc/start'),secondUrl=new URL(second.headers.location!);
    assert.equal((await f.inject({url:'/auth/oidc/callback?'+new URLSearchParams({state:secondUrl.searchParams.get('state')!,code:'single-code'}),cookies:{chm_session:second.cookies[0]!.value}})).statusCode,401);assert.equal(exchanges,1);
  } finally {await f.close();s.close();}
});
test('wrong PKCE or nonce cannot create identity; one-time flow stays consumed on failure',async()=>{
  for (const mode of ['pkce','nonce']) {
    const s=new Store(':memory:');const auth=new Auth(s,config,{jwks,exchange:async()=>{if(mode==='pkce')throw new Error('PKCE rejected');return token('wrong-nonce');}});
    try {const start=auth.start(),url=new URL(start.url),state=url.searchParams.get('state');await assert.rejects(()=>auth.callback(start.raw,state,'code-'+mode));await assert.rejects(()=>auth.callback(start.raw,state,'code-'+mode));assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM players').get() as {n:number}).n,0);}finally{s.close();}
  }
});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync,statSync,writeFileSync,readFileSync,existsSync,symlinkSync,chmodSync} from 'node:fs';
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import {backupToFile} from '../apps/server/src/backup-file.js';
import {add,choice,command} from './helpers.js';

test('Online Backup is private from creation under umask 022 and refuses existing paths',async()=>{
  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-private-backup-')),s=new Store(join(dir,'source.sqlite')),target=join(dir,'copy.sqlite'),previous=process.umask(0o022);
  try {
    const id=add(s);command(s,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]});const before=s.summary('111');let modeAtOpen=-1;
    await backupToFile({async backup(path){modeAtOpen=statSync(path).mode&0o777;return s.db.backup(path);}},target);
    assert.equal(modeAtOpen,0o600);assert.equal(statSync(target).mode&0o777,0o600);
    const restored=new Store(target);try {assert.deepEqual(restored.summary('111'),before);assert.equal(command(restored,{type:'undo'}).spent,'0');}finally{restored.close();}
    const existing=join(dir,'existing');writeFileSync(existing,'must-stay');
    await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(s.db,existing),{code:'EEXIST'});assert.equal(readFileSync(existing,'utf8'),'must-stay');
    const link=join(dir,'link');symlinkSync(existing,link);await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(s.db,link),{code:'EEXIST'});assert.equal(readFileSync(existing,'utf8'),'must-stay');
    const failed=join(dir,'failed');await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile({async backup(){throw new Error('fixture failure');}},failed),/fixture failure/);assert.equal(existsSync(failed),false);
  }finally{process.umask(previous);s.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
});

test('backup rejects trimmed path aliases without overwriting existing data or creating a misleading file',async()=>{
  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-backup-path-')),sourcePath=join(dir,'source.sqlite'),source=new Store(sourcePath),target=join(dir,'existing.sqlite');
  try {
    source.player('111');const existing=new Store(target);existing.player('222');existing.close();chmodSync(target,0o644);
    const before=readFileSync(target);
    for(const suffix of [' ','\t','\n','\u00a0']) {
      await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(source.db,target+suffix),/surrounding whitespace/);
      assert.equal(existsSync(target+suffix),false);assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(target),before);assert.equal(statSync(target).mode&0o777,0o644);
    }
    const cli=fileURLToPath(new URL('../apps/server/dist/backup.js',import.meta.url));
    const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,sourcePath,' existing.sqlite'],{cwd:dir,encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000});
    assert.equal(result.status,1);assert.match(result.stderr,/surrounding whitespace/);assert.equal(existsSync(join(dir,' existing.sqlite')),false);assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(target),before);
    const valid=join(dir,'copy with spaces.sqlite');await backupToFile(source.db,valid);assert.equal(statSync(valid).mode&0o777,0o600);
    const restored=new Store(valid);try {assert.deepEqual(restored.db.prepare('SELECT owner FROM players').all(),[{owner:'111'}]);}finally{restored.close();}
  }finally{source.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {test,expect} from '@playwright/test';
test('mobile shell, no anonymous data or browser persistence',async({page,request})=>{
  await page.route('https://telegram.org/**',route=>route.abort());
  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(page.getByRole('status')).toHaveText('Вход через Telegram пока не настроен. Личные данные недоступны.');
  expect(await page.evaluate(()=>document.documentElement.scrollWidth)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(360);
  expect(await page.evaluate(()=>[localStorage.length,sessionStorage.length])).toEqual([0,0]);
  expect((await request.get('/api/state')).status()).toBe(401);
  expect((await request.post('/api/commands',{data:{}})).status()).toBe(401);
});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {Worker} from 'node:worker_threads';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import {add,choice} from './helpers.js';

test('two worker threads contend on independent SQLite connections and commit exactly once',{timeout:20000},async()=>{
  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-workers-')),path=join(dir,'db.sqlite'),setup=new Store(path),id=add(setup),initial=setup.summary('111');setup.close();
  const gate=new SharedArrayBuffer(4),workers:Worker[]=[];
  try {
    const results=await new Promise<{ok:boolean;error?:string}[]>((resolve,reject)=>{
      let ready=0;const results:{ok:boolean;error?:string}[]=[];
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++) {
        const worker=new Worker(new URL('./fixtures/command-worker.mjs',import.meta.url),{workerData:{path,gate,command:{key:randomUUID(),revision:initial.revision,experiment:initial.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]}}}});workers.push(worker);
        worker.on('error',reject);
        worker.on('message',(message:{ready?:boolean;done?:boolean;ok?:boolean;error?:string})=>{
          if(message.ready&&++ready===2){Atomics.store(new Int32Array(gate),0,1);Atomics.notify(new Int32Array(gate),0,2);}
          if(message.done){results.push({ok:message.ok===true,error:message.error});if(results.length===2)resolve(results);}
        });
      }
    });
    assert.equal(results.filter(r=>r.ok).length,1);assert.equal(results.filter(r=>r.error==='REVISION_CONFLICT').length,1);
    const restored=new Store(path);try {
      assert.equal(restored.summary('111').revision,(BigInt(initial.revision)+1n).toString());assert.equal(restored.summary('111').spent,'84');
      assert.equal(restored.group('111','work',id).quantity,'3');assert.equal(restored.inventory('111','work').length,2);
      assert.equal((restored.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM history').get() as {n:number}).n,1);
    }finally{restored.close();}
  }finally{await Promise.all(workers.map(w=>w.terminate()));rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {parentPort,workerData} from 'node:worker_threads';
import {Store} from '../../apps/server/dist/store.js';

const store=new Store(workerData.path),gate=new Int32Array(workerData.gate);
try {
  parentPort.postMessage({ready:true});
  if(Atomics.wait(gate,0,0,10000)==='timed-out')throw new Error('fixture barrier timeout');
  try {store.command('111',workerData.command);parentPort.postMessage({done:true,ok:true});}
  catch(e){parentPort.postMessage({done:true,ok:false,error:e.code??e.message});}
}finally{store.close();}
~~~~~~

### tests/helpers.ts

Источник `4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b:tests/helpers.ts`; SHA-256 `f6c86011c3c1e67de72af0f307bc34cc8ee6adb4e5b873dabef80a7a8d29f1d9`.

~~~~~~text
import {createHmac,randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import type {Config} from '../apps/server/src/auth.js';
export const config: Config={baseUrl:'http://localhost:3000',sessionSecret:'isolated-fixture-session-secret-32-characters',botToken:'fixture-bot-token',clientId:'fixture-client',clientSecret:'fixture-client-secret',version:'a'.repeat(40)};
export function initData(id: number|string=111, date=Math.floor(Date.now()/1000), extra: Record<string,string>={}) {
  const p=new URLSearchParams({auth_date:String(date),user:JSON.stringify({id,first_name:'Fixture'}),query_id:randomUUID(),...extra});
  const check=[...p.entries()].sort(([a],[b])=>a<b?-1:a>b?1:0).map(([k,v])=>k+'='+v).join('\n');
  const secret=createHmac('sha256','WebAppData').update(config.botToken!).digest();
  p.set('hash',createHmac('sha256',secret).update(check).digest('hex'));return p.toString();
}
export function command(store: Store, action: Record<string,unknown>, owner='111') {
  const s=store.summary(owner); return store.command(owner,{key:randomUUID(),revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action});
}
export function add(store: Store,quantity='6',kind='🅾',tier=1,value='10',owner='111') {
  return command(store,{type:'original.put',kind,tier,value,quantity},owner).createdId!;
}
export const choice=(id:string,quantity='3',worn='0')=>({id,quantity,worn});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~text
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {craft,integer,mod,stats,kinds,type Kind} from '@chm/rules';
test('all 20 prices and every tier transition match PRODUCT constants',()=>{
  const expected={ '🅾':['84','252','756','2268','6804'], '💢':['66','198','594','1782','5346'], '💚':['54','162','486','1458','4374'], '🧿':['72','216','648','1944','5832'] };
  const chains: [Kind,string[]][]=[['🅾',['10','15','23','35','53','80']],['🅾',['11','17','26','39','59','89']],['🅾',['12','18','27','41','62','93']],['💢',['8','12','18','27','41','62']],['🧿',['8','12','18','27','41','62']],['💚',['1','2','3','5','8','12']]];
  for (const [kind,values] of chains) for(let tier=1;tier<=5;tier++) {
    const input={kind,tier,value:values[tier-1]!};
    assert.deepEqual(craft([input,input,input]),{result:{kind,tier:tier+1,value:values[tier]},price:expected[kind][tier-1]});
  }
  assert.equal(craft(['10','11','12'].map(value=>({kind:'🅾',tier:1,value}))).result.value,'17');
  assert.equal(craft(['10','10','11'].map(value=>({kind:'🅾',tier:1,value}))).result.value,'16');
});
test('stats round once after adding percentages and enforce 100 equipped',()=>{
  assert.deepEqual(stats('200',[]),{damage:'200',health:'200'});
  assert.deepEqual(stats('200',(['🅾','💢','🧿'] as Kind[]).map(kind=>({kind,value:kind==='🅾'?'10':'8',quantity:'1'}))),{damage:'252',health:'200'});
  assert.equal(stats('101',[{kind:'💢',value:'8',quantity:'1'}]).damage,'110');
  assert.equal(stats('101',[{kind:'💢',value:'8',quantity:'2'}]).damage,'118');
  assert.equal(stats('200',[{kind:'🅾',value:'10',quantity:'100'}]).damage,'2200');
  assert.equal(stats('200',[{kind:'💚',value:'10',quantity:'1'}]).health,'220');
  assert.throws(()=>stats('200',[{kind:'🅾',value:'10',quantity:'101'}]));
  const big='9007199254740993';
  assert.deepEqual(stats(big,[{kind:'💚',value:big,quantity:'1'}]),{damage:big,health:'811296384146076004301129887254'});
});
test('strict decimal validation and invalid craft combinations',()=>{
  for (const bad of [0,1,1.5,null,{},'-1','0','1.2','1e3','+1','01',' 1','NaN']) assert.throws(()=>integer(bad));
  for (const kind of kinds) for (const tier of [0,7,1.1,'1']) assert.throws(()=>mod({kind,tier,value:'10'}));
  for (const [kind,value] of [['🅾','13'],['💢','9'],['🧿','10'],['💚','0'],['bad','1']]) assert.throws(()=>mod({kind,tier:1,value}));
  for (const kind of kinds) for(let tier=2;tier<=6;tier++) assert.equal(mod({kind,tier,value:'9007199254740993'}).value,'9007199254740993');
  const a={kind:'🅾' as const,tier:1,value:'10'};
  assert.throws(()=>craft([a,a]));assert.throws(()=>craft([a,a,{...a,kind:'💢',value:'8'}]));
  assert.throws(()=>craft([a,a,{...a,tier:2}])); assert.throws(()=>craft([a,a,a].map(m=>({...m,tier:6}))));
});
~~~~~~
