~~~~~~text
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
import {add,choice,command} from './helpers.js';
test('two crafts, repeated undo, worn selection and current level survive SQLite reopen and WAL backup',async()=>{
  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-'));let s=new Store(join(dir,'db.sqlite'));
  try {
    const id=add(s);command(s,{type:'level',value:'200'});
    command(s,{type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:'3'}]});
    const state=s.summary('111'), envelope={key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[choice(id,'3','3')]}};
    const first=s.command('111',envelope); assert.equal(first.spent,'84');assert.deepEqual(first.equipment,[]);
    assert.equal(s.inventory('111','original')[0]!.quantity,'6');
    assert.equal(s.inventory('111','work').find(g=>g.id===id)!.quantity,'3');
    // Discarded HTTP response is modelled by ignoring the first result and replaying its exact envelope.
    assert.deepEqual(s.command('111',envelope),first);assert.equal(s.summary('111').revision,first.revision);
    assert.throws(()=>s.command('111',{...envelope,action:{type:'undo'}}),/KEY_REUSED/);
    const second=command(s,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]});assert.equal(second.spent,'168');
    command(s,{type:'level',value:'101'});command(s,{type:'equip',items:[{id:second.createdId,quantity:'1'}]});
    const before=s.summary('111'); await s.db.backup(join(dir,'backup.sqlite'));s.close();
    s=new Store(join(dir,'db.sqlite'));assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);assert.deepEqual(s.command('111',envelope),first);
    assert.equal(command(s,{type:'undo'}).spent,'84');const undone=command(s,{type:'undo'});
    assert.equal(undone.spent,'0');assert.equal(undone.level,'101');assert.deepEqual(undone.equipment,[{id,quantity:'3'}]);assert.equal(s.inventory('111','work')[0]!.quantity,'6');
    const restored=new Store(join(dir,'backup.sqlite'));
    try {assert.deepEqual(restored.summary('111'),before);assert.equal(command(restored,{type:'undo'}).spent,'84');assert.equal(command(restored,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]}).spent,'168');}finally{restored.close();}
  } finally {s.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
});
test('source changes are isolated; reset clears history, equipment and expenses, changes experiment but keeps level',()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:');try {
    const id=add(s);command(s,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]});command(s,{type:'level',value:'9007199254740993'});
    command(s,{type:'original.put',id,kind:'💚',tier:6,value:'9007199254740993',quantity:'9007199254740993'});
    assert.equal(s.group('111','work',id).quantity,'3');assert.equal(s.group('111','work',id).kind,'🅾');
    const old=s.summary('111'), reset=command(s,{type:'reset'});
    assert.equal(reset.spent,'0');assert.equal(reset.lastCraft,null);assert.deepEqual(reset.equipment,[]);assert.equal(reset.level,'9007199254740993');assert.notEqual(reset.experiment,old.experiment);
    assert.equal(s.group('111','work',id).quantity,'9007199254740993');assert.equal(s.group('111','work',id).value,'9007199254740993');
    assert.throws(()=>s.command('111',{key:randomUUID(),revision:reset.revision,experiment:old.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'5'}}),/REVISION_CONFLICT/);
    command(s,{type:'original.delete',id});assert.equal(s.inventory('111','work').length,1);command(s,{type:'reset'});
    assert.deepEqual(s.inventory('111','work'),[]);assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111').stats,{damage:'9007199254740993',health:'9007199254740993'});
  }finally{s.close();}
});
test('failed commands and injected write failure roll back every part of craft',()=>{
  let fail=false;const s=new Store(':memory:',()=>{if(fail)throw new Error('injected database failure');});try {
    const id=add(s,'101');command(s,{type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:'100'}]});const before=s.summary('111'),groups=s.inventory('111','work');
    for(const action of [
      {type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:'101'}]}, {type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:'1'},{id,quantity:'1'}]},
      {type:'craft',items:[choice(id,'3','0')]}, {type:'craft',items:[choice(id,'2','2'),choice(id,'1','1')]},
      {type:'craft',items:[choice('other')]}, {type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:1,value:'1.5',quantity:'1'},
      {type:'craft',items:[choice(id,'3','4')]}, {type:'craft',items:[choice(id,'2','2')]}
    ]) {assert.throws(()=>command(s,action));assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);assert.deepEqual(s.inventory('111','work'),groups);}
    fail=true;assert.throws(()=>command(s,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id,'3','3')]}),/injected/);
    assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);assert.deepEqual(s.inventory('111','work'),groups);
    assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM history').get() as {n:number}).n,0);
  }finally{s.close();}
});
test('sequential stale revisions across connections conflict; quantities stay aggregated and keys are scoped',async()=>{
  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-'));const a=new Store(join(dir,'db')),b=new Store(join(dir,'db'));
  try {
    const id=add(a,'1000000'),initial=a.summary('111'),key=randomUUID();
    const replies=await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({length:10},(_,i)=>Promise.resolve().then(()=>(i%2?a:b).command('111',{key:randomUUID(),revision:initial.revision,experiment:initial.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]}}))));
    assert.equal(replies.filter(r=>r.status==='fulfilled').length,1);assert.equal(replies.filter(r=>r.status==='rejected'&&/REVISION_CONFLICT/.test(String(r.reason))).length,9);
    assert.equal(a.summary('111').spent,'84');assert.equal(a.group('111','work',id).quantity,'999997');assert.equal(a.inventory('111','work').length,2);
    for (const owner of ['111','222']) {const s=a.summary(owner);a.command(owner,{key,revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:owner}});}
    assert.equal(a.summary('111').level,'111');assert.equal(a.summary('222').level,'222');assert.throws(()=>b.group('222','work',id));
  }finally{a.close();b.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
});
test('derived 129-digit mods remain exact, equippable, craftable and reversible',()=>{
  const s=new Store(':memory:');try {
    const id=add(s,'9','💚',2,'9'.repeat(128)),outputs:string[]=[];
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++)outputs.push(command(s,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]}).createdId!);
    const expected='14'+'9'.repeat(127);
    for(const output of outputs)assert.equal(s.group('111','work',output).value,expected);
    command(s,{type:'equip',items:[{id:outputs[0],quantity:'1'}]});
    const result=command(s,{type:'craft',items:outputs.map((id,i)=>choice(id,'1',i===0?'1':'0'))});
    assert.equal(s.group('111','work',result.createdId).value,'224'+'9'.repeat(126));assert.deepEqual(result.equipment,[]);
    command(s,{type:'equip',items:[{id:result.createdId,quantity:'1'}]});command(s,{type:'level',value:'200'});
    const undone=command(s,{type:'undo'});assert.equal(undone.level,'200');assert.deepEqual(undone.equipment,[{id:outputs[0],quantity:'1'}]);assert.equal(s.group('111','work',outputs[0]).value,expected);
    const reset=command(s,{type:'reset'});assert.equal(reset.spent,'0');assert.equal(reset.level,'200');assert.deepEqual(reset.equipment,[]);assert.equal(s.group('111','work',id).value,'9'.repeat(128));
  }finally{s.close();}
});
~~~~~~

~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/apps/server/src/backup-file.ts b/apps/server/src/backup-file.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3aca13c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/server/src/backup-file.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,19 @@
+import {closeSync, fchmodSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, openSync, unlinkSync} from 'node:fs';
+
+export async function backupToFile(db: {backup(path: string): Promise<unknown>}, target: string) {
+  // better-sqlite3 trims filenames. Exclusive creation and SQLite must address
+  // the same path, otherwise whitespace can alias an existing destination.
+  if (target !== target.trim()) throw new TypeError('Backup target must not have surrounding whitespace');
+  // Exclusive creation prevents overwriting an existing target, including a
+  // symlink. Permissions are private before SQLite opens the destination.
+  const fd=openSync(target,'wx',0o600), created=fstatSync(fd);
+  try {
+    fchmodSync(fd,0o600);
+    await db.backup(target);
+  } catch (error) {
+    // Remove only the file this operation created, never a replaced path.
+    try {const current=lstatSync(target);if(current.dev===created.dev&&current.ino===created.ino)unlinkSync(target);}
+    catch (cleanup) {if (!(cleanup && typeof cleanup==='object' && 'code' in cleanup && cleanup.code==='ENOENT'))throw new AggregateError([error,cleanup],'Backup and cleanup failed',{cause:cleanup});}
+    throw error;
+  } finally {closeSync(fd);}
+}

diff --git a/packages/rules/src/index.ts b/packages/rules/src/index.ts
index a165d17..270918d 100644
--- a/packages/rules/src/index.ts
+++ b/packages/rules/src/index.ts
@@ -12,0 +13,8 @@ export function integer(value: unknown, zero = false): bigint {
+// Only untrusted command fields use this size bound. Derived valid game
+// values may grow past it and continue to use the exact integer() helper.
+export const MAX_INPUT_INTEGER_DIGITS = 128;
+export function inputInteger(value: unknown, zero = false): bigint {
+  requireThat(typeof value === 'string', 'INVALID_INTEGER');
+  requireThat(value.length <= MAX_INPUT_INTEGER_DIGITS, 'INTEGER_TOO_LONG');
+  return integer(value, zero);
+}
diff --git a/scripts/check-measure-pipeline.sh b/scripts/check-measure-pipeline.sh
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..23e7c76
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/check-measure-pipeline.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+probe_dir="$(mktemp -d)"
+trap 'rm -rf "$probe_dir"' EXIT
+cat > "$probe_dir/package.json" <<'JSON'
+{"private":true,"scripts":{"measure":"node -e \"process.exit(17)\""}}
+JSON
+# Match GitHub's explicit bash shell and the actual measurement pipeline.
+if (cd "$probe_dir" && bash --noprofile --norc -e -o pipefail -c 'npm run measure | tee capacity.txt'); then
+  echo 'Measurement failure was hidden by the pipeline' >&2
+  exit 1
+else
+  status=$?
+fi
+printf '{"measurementFailureProbe":"passed","exitCode":%s}\n' "$status"

+      ['application/xml','<command/>',415,'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE'],
+      ['text/plain','not-a-command',400,'INVALID_COMMAND']
+    ] as const) {
+      const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers:{...headers,'content-type':type},payload:body});
+      assert.equal(r.statusCode,status);assert.deepEqual(r.json(),{error});assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);
+    }
+    assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get() as {n:number}).n,0);
+  } finally {await f.close();s.close();}
+});
+test('untrusted numeric fields are bounded before conversion; oversized request leaves both players usable',async()=>{
+  const s=new Store(':memory:'),f=await app(s,config),session=new Auth(s,config).create('111');
+  const headers={origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf},cookies={chm_session:session.raw};
+  const envelope=(action:Record<string,unknown>,revision=s.summary('111').revision)=>({key:randomUUID(),revision,experiment:s.summary('111').experiment,action});
+  try {
+    const accepted=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload:envelope({type:'level',value:'9'.repeat(128)})});assert.equal(accepted.statusCode,200);
+    const created=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload:envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:'9'.repeat(128),quantity:'9'.repeat(128)})});assert.equal(created.statusCode,200);
+    const id=created.json().createdId,before=s.summary('111'),count=(s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get() as {n:number}).n,over='9'.repeat(129);
+    const cases=[envelope({type:'level',value:over}),envelope({type:'level',value:'2'},over),envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:over,quantity:'1'}),envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:'1',quantity:over}),envelope({type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:over}]}),envelope({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:over}]}),envelope({type:'level',value:'9'.repeat(1000000)})];
+    for (const payload of cases) {
+      assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(payload))<1024*1024);
+      const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies,headers,payload});assert.equal(r.statusCode,400);assert.deepEqual(r.json(),{error:'INTEGER_TOO_LONG'});assert.deepEqual(s.summary('111'),before);
+      assert.equal((s.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get() as {n:number}).n,count);
+    }
+    const other=new Auth(s,config).create('222'),state=s.summary('222');
+    const r=await f.inject({method:'POST',url:'/api/commands',cookies:{chm_session:other.raw},headers:{origin:config.baseUrl,'x-csrf-token':other.csrf},payload:{key:randomUUID(),revision:state.revision,experiment:state.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'200'}}});
+    assert.equal(r.statusCode,200);assert.equal(r.json().level,'200');
+  } finally {await f.close();s.close();}
+});

+import {test} from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {mkdtempSync,rmSync,statSync,writeFileSync,readFileSync,existsSync,symlinkSync,chmodSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
+import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
+import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
+import {join} from 'node:path';
+import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
+import {backupToFile} from '../apps/server/src/backup-file.js';
+import {add,choice,command} from './helpers.js';
+
+test('Online Backup is private from creation under umask 022 and refuses existing paths',async()=>{
+  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-private-backup-')),s=new Store(join(dir,'source.sqlite')),target=join(dir,'copy.sqlite'),previous=process.umask(0o022);
+  try {
+    const id=add(s);command(s,{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]});const before=s.summary('111');let modeAtOpen=-1;
+    await backupToFile({async backup(path){modeAtOpen=statSync(path).mode&0o777;return s.db.backup(path);}},target);
+    assert.equal(modeAtOpen,0o600);assert.equal(statSync(target).mode&0o777,0o600);
+    const restored=new Store(target);try {assert.deepEqual(restored.summary('111'),before);assert.equal(command(restored,{type:'undo'}).spent,'0');}finally{restored.close();}
+    const existing=join(dir,'existing');writeFileSync(existing,'must-stay');
+    await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(s.db,existing),{code:'EEXIST'});assert.equal(readFileSync(existing,'utf8'),'must-stay');
+    const link=join(dir,'link');symlinkSync(existing,link);await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(s.db,link),{code:'EEXIST'});assert.equal(readFileSync(existing,'utf8'),'must-stay');
+    const failed=join(dir,'failed');await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile({async backup(){throw new Error('fixture failure');}},failed),/fixture failure/);assert.equal(existsSync(failed),false);
+  }finally{process.umask(previous);s.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+});
+
+test('backup rejects trimmed path aliases without overwriting existing data or creating a misleading file',async()=>{
+  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-backup-path-')),sourcePath=join(dir,'source.sqlite'),source=new Store(sourcePath),target=join(dir,'existing.sqlite');
+  try {
+    source.player('111');const existing=new Store(target);existing.player('222');existing.close();chmodSync(target,0o644);
+    const before=readFileSync(target);
+    for(const suffix of [' ','\t','\n','\u00a0']) {
+      await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(source.db,target+suffix),/surrounding whitespace/);
+      assert.equal(existsSync(target+suffix),false);assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(target),before);assert.equal(statSync(target).mode&0o777,0o644);
+    }
+    const cli=fileURLToPath(new URL('../apps/server/dist/backup.js',import.meta.url));
+    const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,sourcePath,' existing.sqlite'],{cwd:dir,encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000});
+    assert.equal(result.status,1);assert.match(result.stderr,/surrounding whitespace/);assert.equal(existsSync(join(dir,' existing.sqlite')),false);assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(target),before);
+    const valid=join(dir,'copy with spaces.sqlite');await backupToFile(source.db,valid);assert.equal(statSync(valid).mode&0o777,0o600);
+    const restored=new Store(valid);try {assert.deepEqual(restored.db.prepare('SELECT owner FROM players').all(),[{owner:'111'}]);}finally{restored.close();}
+  }finally{source.close();rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+});
diff --git a/tests/concurrency.test.ts b/tests/concurrency.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..bf2dd92
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/concurrency.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,33 @@
+import {test} from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {Worker} from 'node:worker_threads';
+import {mkdtempSync,rmSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
+import {join} from 'node:path';
+import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
+import {Store} from '../apps/server/src/store.js';
+import {add,choice} from './helpers.js';
+
+test('two worker threads contend on independent SQLite connections and commit exactly once',{timeout:20000},async()=>{
+  const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-workers-')),path=join(dir,'db.sqlite'),setup=new Store(path),id=add(setup),initial=setup.summary('111');setup.close();
+  const gate=new SharedArrayBuffer(4),workers:Worker[]=[];
+  try {
+    const results=await new Promise<{ok:boolean;error?:string}[]>((resolve,reject)=>{
+      let ready=0;const results:{ok:boolean;error?:string}[]=[];
+      for(let i=0;i<2;i++) {
+        const worker=new Worker(new URL('./fixtures/command-worker.mjs',import.meta.url),{workerData:{path,gate,command:{key:randomUUID(),revision:initial.revision,experiment:initial.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[choice(id)]}}}});workers.push(worker);
+        worker.on('error',reject);
+        worker.on('message',(message:{ready?:boolean;done?:boolean;ok?:boolean;error?:string})=>{
+          if(message.ready&&++ready===2){Atomics.store(new Int32Array(gate),0,1);Atomics.notify(new Int32Array(gate),0,2);}
+          if(message.done){results.push({ok:message.ok===true,error:message.error});if(results.length===2)resolve(results);}
+        });
+      }
+    });
+    assert.equal(results.filter(r=>r.ok).length,1);assert.equal(results.filter(r=>r.error==='REVISION_CONFLICT').length,1);
+    const restored=new Store(path);try {
+      assert.equal(restored.summary('111').revision,(BigInt(initial.revision)+1n).toString());assert.equal(restored.summary('111').spent,'84');
+      assert.equal(restored.group('111','work',id).quantity,'3');assert.equal(restored.inventory('111','work').length,2);
+      assert.equal((restored.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM history').get() as {n:number}).n,1);
+    }finally{restored.close();}
+  }finally{await Promise.all(workers.map(w=>w.terminate()));rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+});

@@ -0,0 +1,10 @@
+import {parentPort,workerData} from 'node:worker_threads';
+import {Store} from '../../apps/server/dist/store.js';
+
+const store=new Store(workerData.path),gate=new Int32Array(workerData.gate);
+try {
+  parentPort.postMessage({ready:true});
+  if(Atomics.wait(gate,0,0,10000)==='timed-out')throw new Error('fixture barrier timeout');
+  try {store.command('111',workerData.command);parentPort.postMessage({done:true,ok:true});}
+  catch(e){parentPort.postMessage({done:true,ok:false,error:e.code??e.message});}
+}finally{store.close();}

+    const reset=command(s,{type:'reset'});assert.equal(reset.spent,'0');assert.equal(reset.level,'200');assert.deepEqual(reset.equipment,[]);assert.equal(s.group('111','work',id).value,'9'.repeat(128));
~~~~~~

~~~~~~diff
-Backup использует SQLite Online Backup API, включает согласованное состояние WAL. Не копировать только основной файл живой БД. Новая копия не должна существовать: target эксклюзивно создаётся с правами 0600 до открытия SQLite, в том числе при umask 022; существующий файл или symlink не перезаписывается. Использовать приватный родительский каталог/volume, доступный владельцу процесса (для нового каталога — 0700); права чужих существующих каталогов скрипт не меняет. При ошибке удаляется только созданный этой операцией target. Резервируются также история, результаты команд и сессии.
+Backup использует SQLite Online Backup API, включает согласованное состояние WAL. Не копировать только основной файл живой БД. Новая копия не должна существовать: target эксклюзивно создаётся с правами 0600 до открытия SQLite, в том числе при umask 022; существующий файл или symlink не перезаписывается. Использовать приватный родительский каталог/volume, доступный владельцу процесса (для нового каталога — 0700); права чужих существующих каталогов скрипт не меняет. При ошибке удаляется только созданный этой операцией target. Путь назначения с пробельными символами по краям отклоняется до создания файла: better-sqlite3 обрезает такие символы и иначе может обратиться к другому, уже существующему файлу. Пробелы внутри пути допустимы. Резервируются также история, результаты команд и сессии.

@@ -3,0 +4,3 @@ export async function backupToFile(db: {backup(path: string): Promise<unknown>},
+  // better-sqlite3 trims filenames. Exclusive creation and SQLite must address
+  // the same path, otherwise whitespace can alias an existing destination.
+  if (target !== target.trim()) throw new TypeError('Backup target must not have surrounding whitespace');
~~~~~~

## Дополнительная диагностика Codex

Диагностика выполнялась из корня после verify/build командой `node artifacts/codex-followup-probes.mjs`. Для воспроизведения сохранить следующий блок именно по этому пути (каталог artifacts игнорируется Git). Это скрипт developer, не fixture production API. Все таблицы/процессы/backup создаются в собственном временном каталоге и удаляются.

### artifacts/codex-followup-probes.mjs

Снимок фактически выполненной дополнительной диагностики; SHA-256 `4e1b1a13736db2c4aeac579bd7da6560f9737f3fe054d20b622e692031306045`.

~~~~~~javascript
// Codex developer self-check, synthetic fixtures only; no production endpoint changes.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync,statSync,writeFileSync,readFileSync,existsSync,symlinkSync,unlinkSync,chmodSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {fork} from 'node:child_process';
import {SignJWT,generateKeyPair,exportJWK,createLocalJWKSet,jwtVerify} from 'jose';
import {Store} from '../apps/server/dist/store.js';
import {app} from '../apps/server/dist/app.js';
import {Auth,oidcIdentity,issuer} from '../apps/server/dist/auth.js';
import {backupToFile} from '../apps/server/dist/backup-file.js';
const checkedSha='4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b';
const config={baseUrl:'http://localhost:3000',sessionSecret:'synthetic-codex-followup-session-secret-32',botToken:'synthetic-codex-followup-bot',clientId:'synthetic-client',clientSecret:'synthetic-secret',version:checkedSha};
const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-codex-followup-'));
const report={checkedSha,node:process.version};
try {
  const pair=await generateKeyPair('RS256'),jwks=createLocalJWKSet({keys:[{...await exportJWK(pair.publicKey),kid:'probe'}]});
  const remote={jwks,exchange:async()=>{throw new Error('unused transport');}};
  const now=1900000000,date=new Date(now*1000);
  const token=claims=>new SignJWT({iss:issuer,aud:config.clientId,sub:'different-sub',id:111,nonce:'nonce',iat:now,exp:now+600,...claims}).setProtectedHeader({alg:'RS256',kid:'probe'}).sign(pair.privateKey);
  const future=await token({iat:now+5});
  await assert.rejects(()=>jwtVerify(future,jwks,{issuer,audience:config.clientId,maxTokenAge:'1h',clockTolerance:0,currentDate:date}),/iat/);
  for(const delta of [-3600,0,5,30])assert.equal(await oidcIdentity(await token({iat:now+delta}),'nonce',config,remote,date),'111');
  for(const claims of [{iat:now-3601},{iat:now+31},{exp:now},{exp:now-1},{iat:now+30,exp:now+20},{nbf:now+1},{nonce:'wrong'},{iss:'https://invalid.invalid'},{aud:'wrong-client'},{id:undefined}])await assert.rejects(()=>token(claims).then(t=>oidcIdentity(t,'nonce',config,remote,date)));
  assert.equal(await oidcIdentity(await token({nbf:now}),'nonce',config,remote,date),'111');
  report.F1={result:'passed',oldJoseFutureIatRejected:true,acceptedIatDeltas:[-3600,0,5,30],strictExpiryAndNotBefore:true};

  const store=new Store(join(dir,'http.sqlite')),server=await app(store,config),auth=new Auth(store,config),session=auth.create('111');
  const address=await server.listen({host:'127.0.0.1',port:0});
  const headers={origin:config.baseUrl,cookie:'chm_session='+session.raw,'x-csrf-token':session.csrf,'content-type':'application/json'};
  const snapshot=()=>JSON.stringify(['players','groups','history','commands'].map(t=>store.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${t} ORDER BY rowid`).all()));
  const request=async(path,method='GET',body,extra={})=>{const r=await fetch(address+path,{method,headers:{...headers,...extra},body});return {status:r.status,body:await r.json()};};
  const state=async()=>{const r=await request('/api/state');assert.equal(r.status,200);return r.body;};
  const envelope=async action=>{const s=await state();return {key:randomUUID(),revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action};};
  const send=async action=>{const r=await request('/api/commands','POST',JSON.stringify(await envelope(action)));assert.equal(r.status,200);return r.body;};
  try {
    const baseline=snapshot();
    for(const [type,body,status,error]of [['application/json','{"synthetic-private-marker":',400,'INVALID_REQUEST'],['application/xml','<private-synthetic-command/>',415,'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE'],['text/plain','synthetic-not-a-command',400,'INVALID_COMMAND']]){
      const r=await request('/api/commands','POST',body,{'content-type':type});assert.equal(r.status,status);assert.deepEqual(r.body,{error});assert.equal(snapshot(),baseline);
    }
    const beforeOtherErrors=snapshot(),valid=JSON.stringify(await envelope({type:'level',value:'2'}));
    assert.equal((await request('/api/commands','POST',valid,{cookie:'chm_session=forged'})).status,401);
    assert.equal((await request('/api/commands','POST',valid,{origin:'https://invalid.invalid'})).status,403);
    assert.equal((await request('/api/commands','POST',JSON.stringify(await envelope({type:'original.delete',id:'foreign-or-absent'})))).status,404);
    const conflict=await envelope({type:'level',value:'2'});conflict.experiment='foreign-experiment';assert.equal((await request('/api/commands','POST',JSON.stringify(conflict))).status,409);
    const largeBody=JSON.stringify({padding:'x'.repeat(1024*1024)});assert.equal((await request('/api/commands','POST',largeBody)).status,413);assert.equal(snapshot(),beforeOtherErrors);
    store.db.exec("CREATE TRIGGER codex_fixture_fail BEFORE UPDATE ON players BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'synthetic-private-marker'); END;");
    const failure=await request('/api/commands','POST',valid);assert.equal(failure.status,500);assert.deepEqual(failure.body,{error:'SERVER_ERROR'});assert.equal(snapshot(),beforeOtherErrors);store.db.exec('DROP TRIGGER codex_fixture_fail');
    report.F2={result:'passed',transport:'real HTTP socket',statuses:[400,415,400,401,403,404,409,413,500],fourTablesUnchangedOnEveryFailure:true};

    const max='9'.repeat(128),over='9'.repeat(129),put=await send({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:1,value:max,quantity:'9'}),id=put.createdId;
    await send({type:'level',value:'9007199254740993'});assert.equal((await state()).level,'9007199254740993');
    await send({type:'level',value:max});assert.equal((await state()).level,max);
    const cases=[await envelope({type:'level',value:over}),await envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:1,value:over,quantity:'1'}),await envelope({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:1,value:'1',quantity:over}),await envelope({type:'equip',items:[{id,quantity:over}]}),await envelope({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:over,worn:'0'}]}),await envelope({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:over}]}),await envelope({type:'level',value:'9'.repeat(1000000)})];
    cases.push({...await envelope({type:'level',value:'1'}),revision:over});
    const beforeRejected=snapshot();
    for(const c of cases){const body=JSON.stringify(c);assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(body)<1024*1024);const r=await request('/api/commands','POST',body);assert.equal(r.status,400);assert.deepEqual(r.body,{error:'INTEGER_TOO_LONG'});assert.equal(snapshot(),beforeRejected);}
    const other=auth.create('222'),otherState=store.summary('222');
    const otherReply=await request('/api/commands','POST',JSON.stringify({key:randomUUID(),revision:otherState.revision,experiment:otherState.experiment,action:{type:'level',value:'201'}}),{cookie:'chm_session='+other.raw,'x-csrf-token':other.csrf});assert.equal(otherReply.status,200);
    await send({type:'level',value:'200'});
    const outputs=[];for(let i=0;i<3;i++)outputs.push((await send({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:'0'}]})).createdId);
    const readWork=async()=>(await request('/api/inventory?scope=work')).body.items;
    const expected=(3n*BigInt(max)+1n)/2n;assert.equal(String(expected).length,129);
    for(const output of outputs)assert.equal((await readWork()).find(g=>g.id===output).value,String(expected));
    const equipped=await send({type:'equip',items:[{id:outputs[0],quantity:'1'}]});assert.equal(equipped.stats.health,String(200n+(200n*expected+99n)/100n));
    const advanced=await send({type:'craft',items:outputs.map((id,i)=>({id,quantity:'1',worn:i===0?'1':'0'}))});
    assert.equal((await readWork()).find(g=>g.id===advanced.createdId).value,String((3n*expected+1n)/2n));assert.deepEqual(advanced.equipment,[]);
    const undone=await send({type:'undo'});assert.deepEqual(undone.equipment,[{id:outputs[0],quantity:'1'}]);assert.equal(undone.level,'200');
    const reset=await send({type:'reset'});assert.equal(reset.spent,'0');assert.deepEqual(reset.equipment,[]);assert.equal((await readWork())[0].quantity,'9');
    report.F3={result:'passed',transport:'real HTTP socket',inputBoundaries:[128,129],millionDigitRejectedBeforeSavedMutation:true,anotherPlayerStillUsable:true,derivedDigits:129,readEquipCraftUndoReset:true};

    await send({type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:'0'}]});const saved=await state(),original=(await request('/api/inventory?scope=original')).body.items,work=await readWork();
    const backup=join(dir,'copy.sqlite'),parentMode=statSync(dir).mode&0o777,oldMask=process.umask(0o022);let createdMode;
    try{await backupToFile({async backup(path){createdMode=statSync(path).mode&0o777;return store.db.backup(path);}},backup);}finally{process.umask(oldMask);}
    assert.equal(createdMode,0o600);assert.equal(statSync(backup).mode&0o777,0o600);assert.equal(statSync(dir).mode&0o777,parentMode);
    await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(store.db,backup),{code:'EEXIST'});
    const link=join(dir,'link');symlinkSync(backup,link);await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile(store.db,link),{code:'EEXIST'});
    const partial=join(dir,'partial');await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile({async backup(){throw new Error('synthetic-backup-failure');}},partial),/synthetic-backup-failure/);assert.equal(existsSync(partial),false);
    const replaced=join(dir,'replacement');await assert.rejects(()=>backupToFile({async backup(path){unlinkSync(path);writeFileSync(path,'belongs-to-replacement',{mode:0o644});chmodSync(path,0o644);throw new Error('synthetic-backup-failure');}},replaced),/synthetic-backup-failure/);assert.equal(readFileSync(replaced,'utf8'),'belongs-to-replacement');assert.equal(statSync(replaced).mode&0o777,0o644);
    const restored=new Store(backup);try{assert.deepEqual(restored.summary('111'),saved);assert.deepEqual(restored.inventory('111','original'),original);assert.deepEqual(restored.inventory('111','work'),work);}finally{restored.close();}
    report.F4={result:'passed',linuxModeAtCreation:'0600',umask:'022',existingParentModeUnchanged:true,existingFileAndSymlinkRefused:true,onlyOwnPartialRemoved:true,liveWalCopyExact:true};
  }finally{await server.close();store.close();}

  const path=join(dir,'concurrency.sqlite'),setup=new Store(path),initial=setup.summary('111');
  const added=setup.command('111',{key:randomUUID(),revision:initial.revision,experiment:initial.experiment,action:{type:'original.put',kind:'🅾',tier:1,value:'10',quantity:'6'}}),id=added.createdId,before=setup.summary('111');setup.close();
  const workerFile=join(dir,'worker.mjs');writeFileSync(workerFile,`import {Store} from ${JSON.stringify(new URL('../apps/server/dist/store.js',import.meta.url).href)};const s=new Store(process.argv[2]);process.send({ready:true});process.once('message',()=>{try{const r=s.command('111',JSON.parse(process.argv[3]));process.send({ok:true,revision:r.revision});}catch(e){process.send({ok:false,error:e.code??e.message});}finally{s.close();process.disconnect();}});`);
  const children=[];
  try {
    let ready=0;const outcomes=await Promise.all(Array.from({length:2},()=>new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const child=fork(workerFile,[path,JSON.stringify({key:randomUUID(),revision:before.revision,experiment:before.experiment,action:{type:'craft',items:[{id,quantity:'3',worn:'0'}]}})],{stdio:['ignore','ignore','pipe','ipc'],execArgv:[]});children.push(child);
      let outcome;const timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('process probe timeout')),15000);
      child.once('error',reject);child.on('message',message=>{if(message.ready&&++ready===2)for(const c of children)c.send({start:true});if('ok' in message)outcome=message;});
      child.once('exit',code=>{clearTimeout(timer);if(code===0&&outcome)resolve(outcome);else reject(new Error('process probe failed'));});
    })));
    assert.equal(outcomes.filter(o=>o.ok).length,1);assert.equal(outcomes.filter(o=>o.error==='REVISION_CONFLICT').length,1);
    const reopened=new Store(path);try{assert.equal(reopened.summary('111').spent,'84');assert.equal(reopened.summary('111').revision,String(BigInt(before.revision)+1n));assert.equal(reopened.group('111','work',id).quantity,'3');assert.equal(reopened.inventory('111','work').length,2);assert.equal(reopened.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM history').get().n,1);assert.equal(reopened.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM commands').get().n,2);}finally{reopened.close();}
    report.F5={result:'passed',separateOSProcesses:2,barrier:'both ready then IPC start',commits:1,revisionConflicts:1,historyRows:1,successfulCommandRows:2};
  }finally{for(const c of children)if(c.exitCode===null)c.kill();}
  console.log(JSON.stringify(report,null,2));
}finally{rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});}
~~~~~~

## Исходный пакет отдельной сессии Codex — конец

## Дополнительная диагностика management — исходник

Проверка исполнена отдельно от продуктового дерева на версии293ffb5. Пути импорта отражают два локальных снимка: manager-review-task001 — проверяемый293ffb5, developer-task001 — прежний helper без защиты trim, код которого в отдельном diff Codex. Store собран из проверяемого снимка; используется настоящий better-sqlite3 13.0.3. Файл не добавлен в продуктовую реализацию. SHA-256 исходника `2f037438def1442126e7cc990ee4797ae45c700f5c7a107d336a555d1ee5948b`.

~~~~~~javascript
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {mkdtempSync,rmSync,readFileSync,statSync,existsSync,chmodSync,mkdirSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {randomUUID,createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {createRequire} from 'node:module';
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {Store} from './manager-review-task001/apps/server/dist/store.js';
import {backupToFile as fixed} from './manager-review-task001/apps/server/src/backup-file.ts';
import {backupToFile as old} from './developer-task001/apps/server/src/backup-file.ts';

const require=createRequire(new URL('./manager-review-task001/package.json',import.meta.url));
const Database=require('better-sqlite3');
const driverVersion=require('better-sqlite3/package.json').version;
assert.equal(driverVersion,'13.0.3');
const dir=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'chm-manager-f4-'));
const previousCwd=process.cwd(),previousUmask=process.umask(0o022);
let source;
const observations=[];
const owners=path=>{const db=new Database(path,{readonly:true});try{return db.prepare('SELECT owner FROM players ORDER BY owner').all().map(x=>x.owner);}finally{db.close();}};
const hash=path=>createHash('sha256').update(readFileSync(path)).digest('hex');
try {
  process.chdir(dir);
  const sourcePath=join(dir,'source.sqlite'),victim=join(dir,'existing.sqlite');
  source=new Store(sourcePath);source.player('111');
  const command=action=>{const s=source.summary('111');return source.command('111',{key:randomUUID(),revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action});};
  const group=command({type:'original.put',kind:'💚',tier:2,value:'17',quantity:'9'}).createdId;
  command({type:'reset'});
  const output=command({type:'craft',items:[{id:group,quantity:'3',worn:'0'}]}).createdId;
  command({type:'level',value:'101'});command({type:'equip',items:[{id:output,quantity:'1'}]});
  const expected=source.summary('111');
  const makeVictim=()=>{const s=new Store(victim);s.player('222');s.close();chmodSync(victim,0o644);};
  makeVictim();assert.deepEqual(owners(victim),['222']);
  await old(source.db,victim+' ');
  assert.deepEqual(owners(victim),['111']);
  assert.equal(statSync(victim).mode&0o777,0o644);
  assert.equal(statSync(victim+' ').size,0);
  observations.push({case:'old helper + trimmed driver alias',reproduced:true,victimOwners:['111'],victimMode:'0644',misleadingRawFileBytes:0});
  rmSync(victim+' ');rmSync(victim);
  for(const suffix of ['-wal','-shm'])rmSync(victim+suffix,{force:true});
  makeVictim();const before=hash(victim);let driverCalls=0;
  const real={async backup(path){driverCalls++;return source.db.backup(path);}};
  const whitespace=[' ','\t','\n','\r','\v','\f','\u00a0','\ufeff','\u1680','\u2000','\u2001','\u2002','\u2003','\u2004','\u2005','\u2006','\u2007','\u2008','\u2009','\u200a','\u2028','\u2029','\u202f','\u205f','\u3000'];
  for(const w of whitespace) {
    assert.equal(w.trim(),'');
    for(const path of [victim+w,w+'existing.sqlite']) {
      await assert.rejects(()=>fixed(real,path),/surrounding whitespace/);
      assert.equal(existsSync(path),false);assert.equal(hash(victim),before);assert.equal(statSync(victim).mode&0o777,0o644);
    }
  }
  assert.equal(driverCalls,0);assert.deepEqual(owners(victim),['222']);
  observations.push({case:'fixed helper rejects surrounding whitespace before SQLite',passed:true,cases:whitespace.length*2,driverCalls,victimDataAndModePreserved:true});
  const cli=fileURLToPath(new URL('./manager-review-task001/apps/server/dist/backup.js',import.meta.url));
  for(const path of [' existing.sqlite','existing.sqlite\t','existing.sqlite\u00a0']) {
    const r=spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,sourcePath,path],{cwd:dir,encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000});
    assert.equal(r.status,1);assert.match(r.stderr,/surrounding whitespace/);
    assert.equal(existsSync(path),false);assert.equal(hash(victim),before);assert.equal(statSync(victim).mode&0o777,0o644);
  }
  observations.push({case:'real CLI rejects path aliases',passed:true,cases:3,victimDataAndModePreserved:true});
  mkdirSync(join(dir,'sub folder'),{mode:0o700});
  for(const name of ['copy with spaces.sqlite','copy\u200b.sqlite','sub folder/copy.sqlite']) {
    const path=join(dir,name);await fixed(source.db,path);
    assert.equal(statSync(path).mode&0o777,0o600);
    const restored=new Store(path);
    try {assert.deepEqual(restored.summary('111'),expected);const s=restored.summary('111');assert.equal(restored.command('111',{key:randomUUID(),revision:s.revision,experiment:s.experiment,action:{type:'undo'}}).spent,'0');}
    finally {restored.close();}
  }
  observations.push({case:'valid internal spaces, literal zero-width character and directory spaces',passed:true,cases:3,mode:'0600',exactStateAndUndo:true});
  await assert.rejects(()=>fixed(source.db,victim),{code:'EEXIST'});assert.equal(hash(victim),before);
  const uriLike='file:existing.sqlite?mode=rw';await fixed(source.db,uriLike);
  assert.equal(hash(victim),before);assert.deepEqual(owners(victim),['222']);
  assert.deepEqual(owners(uriLike),['111']);assert.equal(statSync(uriLike).mode&0o777,0o600);
  observations.push({case:'URI-like spelling on the pinned native driver',passed:true,literalDestinationUsed:true,existingDatabasePreserved:true});
  console.log(JSON.stringify({sourceSHA:'293ffb5211b13476fd6021d0f9fa0ae2ee8f3b85',functionalSHA:'4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b',node:process.version,driverVersion,observations,result:'passed'},null,2));
}finally {
  source?.close();process.chdir(previousCwd);process.umask(previousUmask);rmSync(dir,{recursive:true,force:true});
}

~~~~~~

## Дополнительная диагностика management — фактический stdout

Команда `node manager-backup-probe.mjs` → exit0. Все данные синтетические; файлы создавались в приватном временном каталоге, удалённом в finally.

~~~~~~json
{
  "sourceSHA": "293ffb5211b13476fd6021d0f9fa0ae2ee8f3b85",
  "functionalSHA": "4cdcd2e36b1c096dd6057f83c3ce66e8f5879c4b",
  "node": "v24.22.1",
  "driverVersion": "13.0.3",
  "observations": [
    {
      "case": "old helper + trimmed driver alias",
      "reproduced": true,
      "victimOwners": [
        "111"
      ],
      "victimMode": "0644",
      "misleadingRawFileBytes": 0
    },
    {
      "case": "fixed helper rejects surrounding whitespace before SQLite",
      "passed": true,
      "cases": 50,
      "driverCalls": 0,
      "victimDataAndModePreserved": true
    },
    {
      "case": "real CLI rejects path aliases",
      "passed": true,
      "cases": 3,
      "victimDataAndModePreserved": true
    },
    {
      "case": "valid internal spaces, literal zero-width character and directory spaces",
      "passed": true,
      "cases": 3,
      "mode": "0600",
      "exactStateAndUndo": true
    },
    {
      "case": "URI-like spelling on the pinned native driver",
      "passed": true,
      "literalDestinationUsed": true,
      "existingDatabasePreserved": true
    }
  ],
  "result": "passed"
}

~~~~~~

Конец пакета management. Заключение отдельного reviewer ещё не получено; технический код и исходные отзывы этой подготовкой не изменялись.
